(iii) **Join.** `ledger_active` joins on `(viewer, subject_ref {stable_id, surface}, and the quantity where the ref's PF-9 triple carries one)` through the published `lie_targets(ref)` (F-1 `scope`): a lie on the temperature of a three-quantity compartment makes `ledger_active` true for the temperature ref only, and a lie on one surface of a three-surface node makes it true for that surface only. **One degradation rule** (F-1 `scope`; E-DO-49 and AC-DO-163 cite it and restate nothing): a **live row** is an `ACTIVE` row of the viewer that is listed for the viewer (`ledger_listed`, SN-23). Zero live rows on the subject: `scope` is `RESOLVED` (a vacuous join; `ledger_active` is false for every ref of the subject whatever `lie_targets` says, so the gap run between the ledger row leaving `ACTIVE` and the Filter's `lie_id` clearing is `SKEW`, bounded by `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS`, and never reaches row 9 inside the grace). `ledger_listed` unpublished and the subject has one or more `ACTIVE` rows: `UNAVAILABLE` / `NO_RESOLVER` (the listing is unknown, never a guess). Otherwise, where `lie_targets(ref)` is unpublished or ambiguous and the subject has more than one ref, exactly one live row on the subject gives `SUBJECT_SCOPE` (every ref of the subject reads `DELIBERATE` tagged `UNVERIFIED`, none `HONEST`, none verified), and two or more live rows give `UNAVAILABLE` / `NO_RESOLVER`; never a guess *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. Sanity's closed `params` names no surface or quantity (sn:345), so the join rides on `subject_ref`, and the remaining ask is OQ-DO-26 (B). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (iv) **`why`.** `DEFECT_UNCAUSED` carries a `why` sub-tag, one of `NO_LEDGER_DIFF` (`lie_id` 0, not `ledger_active`, `p_int != tv`), `LIE_WITHOUT_LEDGER` (`lie_id != 0`, not `ledger_active`, beyond the skew grace), `RETURN_STALLED` (stall bound or progress floor), `RETURN_GROWTH` (envelope or step bound) and `BINARY_RETURN` (non-zero `disc` on a binary-frame ref in `RETURN`); the `why` is shown on `P-TRUTH`, written to the log and kept in the ring (DO-35). (v) **Missing verification is never the verified class** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. A `DELIBERATE` result the Overlay could not verify (no `lie_intent` for the ref, or `SUBJECT_SCOPE`) is tagged `UNVERIFIED` (why `INTENT_UNKNOWN` or `SUBJECT_SCOPE`) **instead of** `LIE`, with its own NOTE text ("a lie is active on the ledger; the Overlay could not verify it (why); not asserted fair"), and is counted. Under `SUBJECT_SCOPE` the ledger-dependent rows 2, 4, 7, 8 and 9 give way to row 6, so no sibling ref of a lying subject is asserted `HONEST`; rows 3 and 5 still fire, because they read no ledger join. Wherever `defects seen` is shown (the `FULL` header, the strip, the `P-TRUTH` ring row, the `dump` header and the log) the Overlay also shows the `unverified` count (now, and sticky seen) and a **coverage readout**, `checks live: intent n/N, leak-probe n/N, bypass-probe n/N`, where N is the size of the classified set (DO-20) and n the refs for which that record is published (`lie_intent`, `drawn_leak`, `drawn_value`); a `defects seen 0` beside coverage `0/N` reads as "nothing was checkable", never as "nothing is wrong". The log carries it as a `COVERAGE` line at `LAUNCH` and on every change of n (DO-35). *AC group:* classifier (one table-driven row per class).

**DO-22. Render-probe: an optional input.** *Rule:* The Overlay asks In-World Instruments (designed (this run); P-2, accepted by IW-38) for a dev-only `drawn_value(ref)` per corruptible node: the displayed value the node actually drew this frame, as the **pre-snap needle angle in integer millidegrees** (the angle before the pixel snap, so a snapped drawn angle is never compared with an unsnapped perceived one), and a dev-only `drawn_leak(ref)`: the gleam amplitude actually drawn this frame, per mille (DO-21 row 3; both asks go to the Instruments owner with the accepted IW-38 probe, OQ-DO-26). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* When present, the class `DEFECT_BYPASS` (the drawn angle differs from the angle implied by `p.displayed_pm` by more than `LEAK_EPS_MDEG` = `LEAK_EPS_DEG` x 1000 = 250 millidegrees, compared as integers and inclusive: exactly 250 passes) detects a node that skipped the Filter. When absent, the classifier stops at `perceived` versus `true_value` versus ledger, and the row's `bypass_check` field reads `ABSENT`; it is never shown as clean and the row's class is not changed to `UNAVAILABLE` on that account alone. The same holds for an absent `drawn_leak(ref)` and the row's `leak_check` field. A ref whose calibration has `value_hi <= value_lo` or `arc_deg <= 0` cannot be mapped to an angle or a grid: it classifies `UNAVAILABLE` / `NO_RESOLVER` and never reaches a probe verdict. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(accepted by Instruments IW-38; until the probe is live in a build, `bypass_check` reads `ABSENT`.)* *Why:* a node that bypasses the Filter looks exactly like a defect and is invisible without the probe; and this is the only place `LEAK_EPS_DEG` is used (it is a needle-angle epsilon in degrees, not a quantum for mGU, mC, dkPa, permille or bp). *AC group:* classifier.

**DO-23. Non-reference desync classes.** (non-reference classes; registry: `desync_classes`) *Rule:* `P-STATE` shows three classes that do not belong to a ref. `HASH_DRIFT` comes from Save/Load's SL-12 reproduction compare (after the last `post_restore`, before the first `RUNNING` tick) or a G4 mismatch, and names the first differing `block_id` where its owner publishes `block_hash()`. `PERCEPTION_IN_HASH` means the hash differs with the Filter on versus off over an unchanged sim (AC-AT-56 shape) and is **displayed from a test-harness result**, never computed live by the Overlay. `HOST_MIRROR_MISMATCH` is Vertical Slice B (P-6), defined, deferred and shown as `n/a`. *Why:* the index's high-risk row (idx:281) is real desync, not only lies. *AC group:* pages (state).

**DO-24. Sanity page.** *Rule:* `P-SANITY` shows, per `PlayerId`, the values in the page table below. The Overlay displays `force_*` state and never invokes it. A seat whose stage or level was set by `force_stage` or `force_level` carries a `FORCED` tag so that a forced stage is never mistaken for an earned one. The tag is fed by the per-seat `forced` dev diagnostic flag in `sanity.state` (SN-28; granted by Sanity 2026-10-27, OQ-DO-4 resolved); the Overlay never infers a forced seat from the level. *Why:* SN-28 (sn:335-337); the page makes AC-SN-88 readable and unblocks AC-SN-144 (named blocker "deferred: Debug Overlay"). *AC group:* pages (sanity), unblocked sibling ACs.

**DO-25. Attention page.** *Rule:* `P-ATTENTION` shows the values in the page table below. A TellAudit violation, or a `tell_len` below `TELL_MIN_TICKS` on an endogenous tell, is `DEFECT`. In `CALM` the TellAudit panel is labelled "last audit at T=..." so that a stale result is never read as current. The raw `level_mAU` and `tell_remaining` are never *displayed* outside the Overlay root, and `tell_remaining` never reaches a `PRESENTATION` subscriber (AT-16); the token lint is Attention's AC-AT-66, reused by reference. *Why:* Pillar 3 ("shown in the world, never a meter" means the meter exists only here); at:1370: the Debug Overlay and the diagnostics channel are "the one place values may be shown". *AC group:* pages (attention), gating (lint).

**DO-26. Frame page, channels and world debug draws.** *Rule:* `P-FRAME` shows the Pipeline's diagnostic surface (pipe:857). `P-CHANNELS` shows the channel list (id, owner, status `OK` / `PENDING` / `STALE` / `ERROR` / `SUSPENDED` / `REJECTED`, the same six values as the channel status machine in States and Transitions, row count) and the selected channel's rows, plus one toggle per declared world debug draw (Lighting occluder outlines and `r_lit` circles, Emitter cones, Layout rects, Horror true positions, Body sort origins, the Pipeline's own layer-0 toggles at pipe:300 row 11: sort origins, occluders, the look-ahead box, pixel grid, band indices, camera limits, the fractional-transform tree-walk). The operator moves the row cursor over the channels and the toggles with `cursor_next` / `cursor_prev`; `activate` on a channel row selects it and on a toggle row flips it (DO-8). Toggles control the dev-only debug node in layer 0 (pipe:102 note e). **World draws render only in `FULL`** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: leaving `FULL` (to the strip, `HIDDEN` or `OFF`) hides the dev node and stops its draw callbacks, the toggle states are kept and resume on re-entry, so a toggled-on draw, Horror's true positions included, can never persist onto a tester's screen (AC-DO-169); every `draw_line` uses a width of `OVERLAY_DEBUG_DRAW_WIDTH_PX` >= 1 base px (4.7 lines have no feather `[REF breaking-changes.md]`; pipe:923). The `DEBUG_OVERLAY` render-state modifier (pipe:267) is a flag over any state, not a state. **Risk (spike S-4):** layer 0 is the lit world viewport and the Filter's full-screen pass sits on its container (pipe PR-3, PR-10), so a debug node there can be hidden in a dark compartment, jolted by the camera and, at stage 3, graded by the sane-to-flesh hue pass; the Overlay's own world marks could be corrupted by the lie they are meant to expose. MVP keeps the locked layer-0 debug node with draws set unshaded `[UNV]`; a spike measures that draw colours are unchanged under `force_stage` 3. If it fails the fallback is a **layer-4 projection** (the Overlay draws the same outlines in the root viewport through the camera's world-to-screen transform, which the Pipeline would have to expose): a Pipeline amendment request held in Open Questions, not decided here. *Why:* pipe:102 and pipe:104 note e. *AC group:* palette, world draws.

**DO-27. Palette and severity presentation.** *Rule:* The UI palette only: text Paper White `#DDE4EA`, the perceived value Hull Grey `#8C949C`, selection Tungsten `#FFCC66`, plate Ground Graphite `#1C2128` (frame `#5C6670`). Severity is a **text tag** first (`OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN`, `DEFECT`). Alarm Red `#E0102A` appears only as a warning **mark** beside a `DEFECT` tag and for `NO_MASK` (pipe:735), never as a row fill, an accent or a decoration (ab:375 keeps red "exclusively for alarm so rarity is the signal"; ab:463 "only on a failing instrument; never decorative"; a `DEFECT` row is a failing instrument, a `NOTE` is not). `WARN` uses no red. `NOT_USED` (the `PER_ELEMENT` container state) is not red (pf:446, AC-PF-38). Never violet, never flesh, even when the world lie leaks violet (Body :853 asks the same of its debug tint: Hull Grey or a text-only cue). *Why:* Pillar 1 (the sane palette is inviolable; violet and flesh are reserved for sanity leaks). *(orchestrator ruling: Alarm Red narrowed to a mark by ab:375; an art-director-ratifiable note is carried in Open Questions.)* *AC group:* palette.

**DO-28. Legibility and refresh.** *Rule:* Text-first pages on a Graphite plate with fixed-width digits; numerals refresh at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` while markers and debug draws update per frame; a bar, where shown, is **linear with a needle mark** (no ring, no dial; the 2026-10-26 UI-grammar entry in `docs/consistency-failures.md`; ab 3.4). Pages, not floating windows. `PAUSED` freezes `T` but numerals still refresh so a paused session is inspectable. *Why:* the 10 Hz numeral cadence keeps digits readable while per-frame marks stay smooth. *AC group:* palette, pages.

**DO-29. Inertness.** *Rule:* With the Overlay `FULL` versus absent over the same scripted run: the `state_hash` sequence is identical; the Filter's draw path is unchanged (E-PF-61); the Overlay's extra `perceived` calls hit the Filter's per-frame memo and allocate nothing on the honest path; and the frame cost stays under `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (dev builds only). The Overlay reuses the five dependents' absence ACs by reference and adds the accessibility-enumeration, rebinding-enumeration, screen-reader-tree and reserved-hue negatives. *Why:* observation must not change the system (AT-32, AC-AT-56 shape); inertness is the first acceptance theme. *AC group:* inertness.

**DO-30. The Never list.** *Rule:* The Overlay is never: an accessibility option, caption, setting or shortcut (R-7, ab:886); a rebinding entry; a screen-reader (AccessKit) node; a tutorial line or onboarding step; a "developer mode" menu item; an achievement or a telemetry upload; audio or sonification (Audio's voice counters are displayed, never sonified; no beep on `DEFECT`); a release-build presence; a write surface (no submit, mutator, `force_*` button, teleport, spawn, knob slider or rewind); a replay or rewind debugger (the command-log tail is display only; replay is the kernel's G3 harness); an in-game editor (anti-pillar: not a freeform builder). *Why:* guard rails in Player Fantasy; P4 ("an honest overlay on a lying HUD would make Pillar 4 switchable off"). *AC group:* gating, input path, palette.

**DO-31. Evidence capture and marks.** *Rule:* One `dump` action writes a plain-text snapshot of every page, headed by the coverage readout and the `defects seen` and `unverified` counts, `T`, `hash_T`, the algorithm tag (the kernel's `hash_algo` field, DO-17, granted 2026-10-27; `H64 (algo untagged; SHA-256/8)` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings, consistency follow-up)* only if it is absent), the viewer and the mode, to a dev path under `user://debug/` `[PLACEHOLDER]`; the number of retained dumps is capped (`DIAG_DUMP_MAX_FILES`). Screenshots follow `.claude/docs/run-and-observe.md` into `production/qa/evidence/`. One `mark` action stamps the current `T` (up to `DIAG_MARK_MAX`) as a named observer mark; the first `DIAG_MARK_MAX` marks are kept and a further `mark` is refused with a `NOTE` rather than dropping an older stamp (*orchestrator ruling*: a stopwatch start must not vanish silently); `mark` is accepted in every mode; in `HIDDEN` it is silent (no draw, no confirmation line) and is echoed to the observer log, which records every mark while the on-screen stopwatch list keeps the first `DIAG_MARK_MAX` (DO-35); marks are session state that survives a mode change; **mark ordinals have a protocol meaning** (table after DO-35); *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* the stage-entry stopwatch (`stage_entry_latency_s`, Formulas) uses it so an observer can time sanity stage-1 entry from boarding. Streaming and CSV or JSON export remain the AT-32 diagnostics-pipeline ADR. *Why:* AC-SN-88 and AC-BS-122 need retained evidence. *AC group:* evidence.

**DO-32. Settle after restore and boundaries.** *Rule:* On `LOADING` to `RUNNING` (restore, new world, transit) every history window (P99 samples, peaks, silent-lie counters, `RETURN` trackers) is cleared, while the defect ring, the marks and the log survive (DO-35), and the classifier reports `UNAVAILABLE` with reason `SETTLING` for `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` kernel ticks after the first `RUNNING` tick (a pause inside the window holds `SETTLING` until the ticks elapse, because `T` is frozen), so the Filter's presentation-local tails (E-PF-5: a return track dropped by a restore) never read as defects. Opening the Overlay mid-session starts empty windows and says "insufficient samples (n/N)" rather than issuing a verdict. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *Why:* a restore legitimately produces an `ALL` collapse and drops presentation-local state. *AC group:* kernel states.

**DO-33. Kernel-side requests (routed, not edited).** *Rule:* The GDD requested the following of the kernel owner and does not depend on any of them being granted; **(a), (b) and (c) were APPLIED by the kernel owner 2026-10-27** (command-simulation-layer.md: the `diagnostics` accessor carrying `hash_T` and `hash_algo`, `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` in its Tuning Knobs, forced hashes at snapshot, restore and `PAUSED`, kern:915 re-worded, the timer read-only): (a) a read-only `diagnostics` accessor, one snapshot per tick or on request, zero allocation when the Overlay is absent, no effect on `state_hash`, **carrying `hash_T` and a `hash_algo` field** (neither is a kernel-published name today; kern names only `H64`) (P-9; kern:749 lists contents, not an API shape); (b) a kernel knob `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (default 60, range 6 to 600), forced hashes at snapshot and restore (SL-12 already computes one) and on `PAUSED`, with the hash run outside P3 so its time never enters D.6's sum of `t_hook`, and **phased off the class fold** (on a tick whose `T mod 12` is not 0, for example `T mod 60` = 6, since the default cadence 60 is a multiple of 12 and would otherwise land every hash on a fold frame, F-7) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and with kern:915 amended from "refreshed for that tick" to "refreshed at the diagnostics cadence"; (c) the kernel's diagnostics timer exposed read-only as a monotonic microsecond reading in the `diagnostics` accessor (proposed name `diag_clock_us`, **provisional: the kernel owner names it**; the kernel names no accessor member today), allow-listed with the timer file in the H9 lint (kern:781), for the Overlay's own cost row (H9 allow-lists only that timer and the tick driver, kern:781; until answered the self-cost AC is a manual check against the engine profiler). If the kernel declines, the Overlay shows whatever the kernel publishes. *Why:* a DIAGNOSTIC subscriber reads; it cannot schedule kernel work. kern:915 now reads "refreshed at the diagnostics cadence" (applied 2026-10-27). *(orchestrator ruling: the kernel computes, the Overlay displays.)* *AC group:* unblocked sibling ACs (kern:915).

**DO-34. Vertical Slice B contract rows only.** *Rule:* Authoritative values appear on the `HOST`; a `MIRROR` shows only its own seat's true/perceived pair, **and that own-seat pair classifies `UNAVAILABLE` with reason `NO_LEDGER`** (`sanity.ledger` is `HOST_ONLY`, so `ledger_active` cannot be evaluated: the pair is displayed, never judged `HONEST` or `DELIBERATE`); `HOST_MIRROR_MISMATCH` is defined and deferred; `remote_queries` is displayed as the leakage indicator and is expected 0 in MVP; there is no multi-seat panel design and the seat cycler is a contract only. A loopback (two local instances) stub fixture is the only MVP proof. *(provisional — Co-op Session & Replication undesigned.)* *Why:* P-6; scope guard. *AC group:* cross-system.

**DO-35. Observer event log and the defect ring.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; new rule)* *Rule:* The Overlay owns one **non-screen observer sink**, the same stream as the latched defect ring, so the MVP kill/continue playtest (X-BS-11, AC-BS-122) and AC-SN-88 can be measured without the tester's screen ever carrying the Overlay. (1) **Log.** In every mode but `OFF` (a `MARK` line is written in every mode, `OFF` included, because a mark is an explicit observer act) the model hands timestamped plain-text lines to the injected sink, which appends them to `user://debug/observer-<launch_id>.log` (`DIAG_DUMP_DIR`; `launch_id` is supplied by the composition root, the Overlay reads no wall clock, H9), one line per event, `T=<tick> <KIND> key=value ...`, with kinds `LAUNCH`, `MODE`, `KERNEL_STATE` (the `FAULTED` line carries all six FAULT-record fields `T`, `type`, `issuer`, `host_order`, `block_id`, `assertion`), `ATTN_PHASE` (old, new, `tell_epoch`), `SANITY_STAGE` (PlayerId, old, new, `FORCED` flag), `VIEW` (the view's drawn Overlay-node count, at `LAUNCH` and on change; 0 in `HIDDEN`), `HASH` (a status change), `CHANNEL` (a status change to `ERROR`, `SUSPENDED` or `REJECTED`), `MARK` (ordinal, `T`), `DEFECT` (first sight of a class: `ref_key`, viewer, class, `why`, `disc`, `tol_eff`, `d_intended_u`), `LIE` (a ledger-row edge, so a "the gauge lied" report can be answered with "a fair, leaking lie on that ref at `T`" and not only with "no `DEFECT` was logged": `slot`, `lie_id`, `ref_key`, `kind`, `direction`, `leak_modes`, `class`, `tag`; written when a row becomes `ACTIVE`, when the ref's class or first tag changes, and when the row leaves `ACTIVE`) and `COVERAGE` (the coverage readout and the `unverified` and `unclassified` counts, DO-21 (5)(v), at `LAUNCH` and on any change) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Lines are appended to the injected `ObserverSink` (T-7), never written by the model, and the P5 handler stays a pure copy (AC-DO-119). **Flush triggers** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the sink writes (a) at each class fold that has pending lines, never per tick; (b) at once on a `MARK`, in any mode including `OFF` and including while the kernel is `PAUSED` (a frozen `T` has no fold); (c) at once on entry to `PAUSED` or `FAULTED`; (d) at once on the window's `WM_CLOSE_REQUEST`. Writes are batched: a flush is one append of a pre-formatted buffer to an open file, at most one per rendered frame, so a burst of triggers coalesces; triggers (b) to (d) are rare human or edge events and are the only synchronous ones, and the sink may hand the buffer to a worker thread (-> ADR candidate) so a tester machine never takes a stall from the log. The log is capped (`DIAG_LOG_MAX_LINES`, `DIAG_LOG_MAX_FILES`), optionally echoed to stdout (`OVERLAY_LOG_STDOUT`, default false), is the observer's record in the playtest preset, and never reaches a screen. (2) **Marks in `HIDDEN`.** `mark` is accepted silently in `HIDDEN`, drawn nowhere, and echoed as a `MARK` line; the on-screen stopwatch list keeps the first `DIAG_MARK_MAX` marks (DO-31) while the log records every mark, so a stray key press can add a spurious line but can never destroy the real one. (3) **Defect ring.** Each first sight of a `DEFECT` class (an edge into a defect class for a `(ref, viewer)`, not every fold) is also appended to a bounded in-memory ring (`DIAG_DEFECT_RING_MAX`) that **survives `OFF`, a mode change and a restore** (DO-9: dev-session state, cleared only at launch), so a transient defect is not lost with the history windows. `FULL` shows the ring on `P-TRUTH` and a **sticky `defects seen` count** (total and per class) in the header with the `unverified` count and the coverage readout beside it (DO-21 (5)(v)); the strip shows the total, the `unverified` count and the coverage digits *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. (4) **Collection profile.** `HIDDEN` and the strip collect only the observer set: the strip fields, the class folds for the classified set (DO-20; they feed the ring and the log) and the log events; the hook window, the dirty-key peaks, the channel pulls and the `P-FRAME` surface are collected in `FULL` only, so `HIDDEN` adds the least load to the session it is protecting (F-7). *Why:* the Overview says the playtest cannot run without the Overlay, and every screen path is tester-hostile; a file is the only observer channel that leaves the tester's capture clean. *AC group:* observer, evidence.

**Mark ordinals (DO-31, DO-35)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The ordinal is the order of arrival in the session; the observer protocol (OQ-DO-18) presses `mark` in this order:

| Ordinal | Meaning | Read by |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | The boarding (the tester crosses onto the derelict) | F-9 `T_mark`; AC-SN-88; AC-DO-104 |
| 2 | The creak (the hull tell the tester hears) | X-BS-11, AC-BS-122; AC-DO-74 |
| 3 to 8 | Free observer notes; no protocol meaning | the observer only |

An ordinal-1 press that arrives late (after the stage-1 entry) is reported by F-9 as `MARK_AFTER_ENTRY`, never as a negative latency.

**The channel registration contract** (DO-12; proposed registry name `diag_panel_contract`):

| Field | Type / range | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `channel_id` | snake_case string, unique | A collision is a registration error shown on `P-CHANNELS` |
| `owner` | system name | For the channel list and the Cross-References |
| `rows[]` | ordered list of `(name, unit, kind, severity_rule)` | `kind` is one of `COUNTER` (monotonic), `GAUGE`, `FLAG` (bool), `TEXT`, `TABLE` (rows by columns, capped); `unit` is a label, never part of the name |
| `severity_rule` | predicate over the row's value, result in `OK` / `NOTE` / `WARN` / `DEFECT` | Evaluated by the Overlay; a `DEFECT` rule cites the owner's rule it enforces |
| `draws[]` | optional list of `(toggle_id, label)` | World debug-draw toggles; the owner supplies the draw callback for the dev node in layer 0 |
| `pull()` | returns a plain Dictionary `{channel_id, rows, complete}`, no live reference | Called at most at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; the Overlay copies and validates it against `DIAG_ERR` (DO-14); an invalid result is a channel fault *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| limits | `DIAG_CHANNELS_MAX`, `DIAG_ROWS_PER_CHANNEL_MAX` | Exceeding either is a registration error row, not a crash |

**The classifier classes** (DO-21) (registry: `desync_classes`). Evaluation order is the row order; the first match wins; the last row is the catch-all. All inputs are read in the same frame (DO-20).

| # | Class | Severity | Test | Cross-read / note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOTE` | An input is missing. Reasons: `NOT_RUNNING` (kernel not `RUNNING`), `SETTLING` (DO-32), `REMOTE_VIEWER` (DO-10), `NO_LEDGER` (a mirror, DO-16), `NO_RESOLVER` (the ref has no ref-to-subject mapping; or `lie_targets(ref)` is unpublished or ambiguous and the subject has more than one ref and two or more live rows (`ACTIVE` and listed), where exactly one degrades to `SUBJECT_SCOPE` on row 6 and zero is `RESOLVED` (vacuous), F-1 `scope`; or `ledger_listed` is unpublished and the subject has an `ACTIVE` row; or a needle or readout calibration with `value_hi <= value_lo` or `arc_deg <= 0`, never asked of a binary-frame ref), `NO_TRUE_SOURCE` (`SCANNER`), `MEMO_UNCONFIRMED` (a `NO_LEDGER_DIFF` seen on fewer than `DIAG_NO_LEDGER_DIFF_FOLDS` consecutive folds, last in precedence, *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*) | The reason is always shown; when several hold, the one listed first here wins (`NOT_RUNNING`, `SETTLING`, `REMOTE_VIEWER`, `NO_LEDGER`, `NO_RESOLVER`, `NO_TRUE_SOURCE`; *orchestrator ruling*: the most global cause first). **Never** shown as `HONEST`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `NO_RESOLVER` widened)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| 2 | `SKEW` | `NOTE` | `scope` RESOLVED, `ledger_active` XOR (`lie_id != 0` and `motion_mode != RETURN`), with `lie_intent.not_drawn` NONE, for at most `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS` consecutive kernel ticks counted at class folds (`n_skew`, ticks) | Sanity and the Filter are one run apart. Renamed from `PENDING` (collides with Sanity's ledger state `PENDING`, sn:388-389, and the channel status). Independent of whether `lie_id` stays non-zero in `RETURN` (OQ-DO-2). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: grace in ticks, advanced only when `T` changed)* |
| 3 | `DEFECT_NO_LEAK` | `DEFECT` | `lie_id != 0`, `motion_mode != RETURN` and any of: (i) `leak_modes & GLEAM == 0` (a **tripwire** on the Filter's own guarantee, PF-15, which forces GLEAM, so it should never fire); (ii) the Filter's `leak_repaired` counter rose since the previous fold **and** the Filter published the repaired lie's id (`leak_repaired_lie_id`, requested in OQ-DO-26 (A)) and it equals this ref's `lie_id`; the counter is global, so without that id a rise is raised **once per counter edge** as one page-level `UNATTRIBUTED` event (the Filter's `DEFECT` severity, counted once in `defects seen` under `unattributed`, logged as a `DEFECT` line with `ref_key` UNATTRIBUTED) and no ref's class changes, so a rise cannot mark every lying ref `DEFECT_NO_LEAK` and inflate the count at stage 3 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; (iii) the Instruments probe is present, `drawn_leak(ref) == 0` while `leak_amplitude_pm >= floor_eff` (the stage-indexed `AMP_FLOOR_PM`, 400/325/250 at stages 0-1/2/3 for the viewer's sanity stage; registry `lie_leak_amplitude`, cited) for more than `DIAG_NO_LEAK_GRACE_TICKS` ticks (`n_leak`): a gleam that was dropped, fell under the floor or was cut by an FX cap. The `RETURN` exclusion stops a fading return amplitude from reading as a dropped leak | `leak_modes_dropped`, `leak_repaired`, `drawn_leak`; with no probe `leak_check` = `ABSENT`, never clean. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the earlier row tested only the GLEAM bit the Filter guarantees, so real leak failures went undetected. orchestrator ruling kept: the `RETURN` exclusion, added after the checkpoint so the row stays independent of whether `lie_id` stays non-zero in `RETURN`, OQ-DO-2)* | *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*
| 4 | `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` | `DEFECT` | `scope` RESOLVED, `ledger_active`, `lie_intent` published, and either (a) `lie_id != 0` and `n_eq > DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS`, where `n_eq` counts ticks (at class folds) on which all of: `lie_age >= reach_age` (`ceil(GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x (end_run - active_run))` runs; binary-frame refs `SWAP_LEAD_S` x 5), `disc <= tol_eff`, `lie_intent.not_drawn == NONE`, `lie_intent.short_reach` false and `d_int_u > tol_eff` (the Filter *intended* a visible departure and drew none); or (b) `lie_id == 0`, `lie_intent.not_drawn == NONE` and `n_skew > DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS` (the Filter never picked the entry up) | `lie_unrenderable`, `lies_partial`, `lie_short_reach`. The age gate is why a slow `GLIDE` onset is never a false defect; `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` 0.6 `[PLACEHOLDER]` is the Filter's (pf D.2), cited, and by that age arrival at `D_eff >= D_VIS_MIN_DEG` is guaranteed (pf:646). A lie the Filter designed to draw without a needle departure (no room, an intended departure inside the tolerance, an ineligible or unrenderable needle, a true value sweeping through an absolute target) fails the intent conditions and reads `DELIBERATE` with its tag (row 6). With `lie_intent` unpublished, or in `SUBJECT_SCOPE`, the row is suspended (`DELIBERATE` tagged `UNVERIFIED`, F-1 `unverified`). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: both branches used to flag the Filter's designed fallbacks as defects)* |
| 5 | `DEFECT_BYPASS` | `DEFECT` | The probe is present and the drawn pre-snap angle differs from the angle implied by `p.displayed_pm` by more than `LEAK_EPS_MDEG` (250 millidegrees, integer, inclusive) (DO-22) | With no probe, `bypass_check` = `ABSENT`; never clean. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: pre-snap integer comparison)* |
| 6 | `DELIBERATE` | `NOTE`, tagged `LIE` when verified and `UNVERIFIED` when not *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `ledger_active` and either `lie_id != 0` with `leak_modes & GLEAM != 0`, or `lie_id == 0` with `lie_intent.not_drawn != NONE` or `lie_intent` unpublished, or `scope` = `SUBJECT_SCOPE`; and not row 4. Second tag, first match in this order: `NO_NEEDLE`, `SHORT_REACH`, `SUB_TOL`, `ONSET` (the exact `onset` predicate of F-1). First tag: `LIE` only when `lie_intent` is published and `scope` is RESOLVED; otherwise `UNVERIFIED` with its why (`INTENT_UNKNOWN` or `SUBJECT_SCOPE`) and the NOTE text of DO-21 (5)(v) | Shows `lie_kind`, `motion_mode`, `leak_amplitude_pm`, the second tag and `d_int_u`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the tags `SHORT_REACH`, `SUB_TOL`, `NO_NEEDLE` mark the Filter's designed fallbacks; `UNVERIFIED` marks a result the Overlay could not check)* |
| 7 | `RETURNING` | `NOTE` | `motion_mode == RETURN`, not `ledger_active` (no `lie_id` test) and `disc != 0`, `t_stall <= t_return_max(lie)` (published by the Filter per lie, registry `filter_counters`; depends on `rm`), and the closure test of Formulas: the envelope for `t_ret < WANDER_BLEND_S`, then the step test and the windowed progress floor. On a binary-frame ref any non-zero `disc` fails this row | Closes to `HONEST` on the frame `p_int == tv`, even if `motion_mode` is still `RETURN` and whether or not `lie_id` has cleared (row 8). The Filter's own closure (pf:291, CLEAR): `motion_mode` returns to HONEST without an event once the discrepancy falls under `LEAK_EPS_DEG`, and the honest path is then exact, so the return ends at a sub-`LEAK_EPS_DEG` discrepancy and then switches to `displayed == tv` (OQ-DO-20 answered). A return whose true value has moved reads `RETURNING` tagged `TARGET_MOVING` while the follower is still progressing. *(orchestrator ruling: DO-21 reconciliation 1; closure rule added at final authoring after the qa-lead's review found that row 7, as drafted, would have kept matching a closed return.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: stall clock and progress floor)* |
| 8 | `HONEST` | `OK` | Not `ledger_active`, (`lie_id == 0` or `motion_mode == RETURN`) and `p_int == tv` exactly, with `p_int` as defined in F-1 (the honest path's own `displayed_value`; the grid integer from `displayed_pm` otherwise). The `RETURN` clause keeps the row independent of whether `lie_id` stays non-zero until the follower closes (OQ-DO-2) | The Filter's counters are all 0. A closed return whose grid integer equals `tv` is `HONEST`, not `DEFECT_UNCAUSED`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: integer `p_int`)* |
| 9 | `DEFECT_UNCAUSED` | `DEFECT`, with a `why` sub-tag | The catch-all: not `ledger_active`, `lie_id == 0` and `p_int != tv` outside a valid `RETURN` (`NO_LEDGER_DIFF`); `lie_id != 0`, `motion_mode != RETURN`, not `ledger_active`, beyond the skew grace (`LIE_WITHOUT_LEDGER`); a `RETURN` that fails row 7 (`RETURN_STALLED`: stall bound or progress floor; `RETURN_GROWTH`: envelope or step bound; `BINARY_RETURN`: a non-zero `disc` on a binary-frame ref) | A Filter or Instruments bug, or real desync. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the `why` sub-tag)* |

**The seven pages as data tables** (DO-15). Cadence key: `tick` = collected in the handler every kernel tick and peak-held; `R` = refreshed at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; `ev` = on event. "Kernel dx" is the kernel's diagnostics accessor (P-9; the shape is the kernel's or the ADR's, so the labels here are display labels, not API names). Severity rules marked `[PLACEHOLDER]` await their owner's confirmation.

*P-KERNEL*

| Row | Source | Unit | Cadence | Severity rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `T` | kernel dx | ticks, and mm:ss at `TICK_RATE` 60 | tick, R | none |
| Kernel state | kernel dx | enum (`BOOTING`, `LOADING`, `RUNNING`, `PAUSED`, `SAVING`, `FAULTED`) | ev | `FAULTED` = `DEFECT`; others `OK` |
| Queue depth, total | kernel dx | commands against `QUEUE_CAPACITY` 1024 | tick (peak) | `queue_load_pm` at or above `DIAG_QUEUE_WARN_PM` = `WARN` |
| Queue depth, per issuer | kernel dx | commands against `PER_ISSUER_CAPACITY` 256 | tick (peak) | same rule on the worst issuer |
| Command log tail | kernel dx | rows `(tick, issuer, type, result, reject reason)`; `DIAG_CMD_LOG_ROWS` rows | R | rejects `NOTE`; a `QUEUE_FULL` reject `WARN` |
| `log_coverage_ticks` | kernel D.13, cited | ticks, and seconds (`COMMAND_LOG_CAPACITY` 1024) | R | none; the cited worst case is at least 8 ticks |
| Rejection counters by reason | kernel dx | counts | R | a rising count `NOTE` |
| Catch-up lag (`lag_ticks`, kern:351) | kernel dx | ticks against `MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME` 4 | tick (peak) | `lag_ticks` at or above `DIAG_LAG_WARN_TICKS` = `WARN` |
| FAULT record | kernel dx | `(T, type, issuer, host_order, block_id, assertion)` | ev | present = `DEFECT` |
| Boot progress and failure | kernel dx | text | ev | a failure = `DEFECT` |

*P-HOOKS*

| Row | Source | Unit | Cadence | Severity rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Resolved order | kernel dx (kern:607) | rows `(index, hook_id, owner, divisor, offset, after)` | ev | none; an unknown id is a boot `DEFECT` |
| Per-hook `t_hook`, last and window maximum | kernel dx | ms | tick (peak) | none (the kernel owns the threshold) |
| Sum of running `t_hook` this tick and `step_over_budget` | kernel D.6, cited | ms; bool | tick | over = `WARN` |
| Over-budget ticks in the window | model count | ticks | R | more than 0 = `NOTE` |
| `graph_hook_tick`, last | `hook_window_p99` formulas | ms | per Graph hook tick | none |
| `graph_hook_tick`, P99 and maximum over `DIAG_HOOK_WINDOW_RUNS` hook-tick samples | same | ms against `GRAPH_HOOK_BUDGET_MS` (per hook tick) | R | On the Release template: P99 above 0.5, or at least `DIAG_HOOK_MAX_BREACH_RUNS` samples above 1.0 (MVP) = `WARN`; exactly one sample above 1.0 = `NOTE` "1 outlier"; on the dev template "dev template: indicative, no verdict" unless `DIAG_DEV_TEMPLATE_SCALE_PM` calibrates it; fewer than `DIAG_P99_MIN_SAMPLES` shows "insufficient samples (n/N)" *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Hook rate | kernel D.2, cited | Hz = `TICK_RATE / divisor` | ev | none |

*P-STATE*

| Row | Source | Unit | Cadence | Severity rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `state_hash`, `hash_T`, age, algorithm tag (the kernel's `hash_algo` field, DO-33a, granted 2026-10-27; `H64 (algo untagged; SHA-256/8)` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings, consistency follow-up)* only if the field is absent) | kernel dx (D.12) | u64 hex; ticks | R | age: `NOTE` beyond 2x `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` (the kernel's published hash cadence, accepted 2026-10-27, DO-33b); an unknown `hash_algo` = `WARN` |
| Per-block table | block registry (CR-8) | `block_id`, owner, class, `schema_version`, persist scope | ev | none |
| Per-block hash | owner `block_hash()`; `n/a` otherwise; the K-1 column if adopted | u64 hex, with the tick it was taken | R | `n/a` = `NOTE`, not `DEFECT` |
| Dirty keys per block | P5 batch | keys; `dirty_load_pm` | tick (peak) | `WARN` at or above `DIAG_DIRTY_WARN_PM` |
| `ALL` collapses | P5 batch | count | tick | more than 0 = `WARN`, annotated `post_restore` when one follows a restore |
| `HASH_DRIFT` | Save/Load SL-12 compare, G4 harness | enum; first differing `block_id` | ev | drift = `DEFECT` |
| `PERCEPTION_IN_HASH` | test-harness result | enum; `n/a` live | ev | true = `DEFECT` |
| Save status | Save/Load channel (`save_status`, last generation, `written_at_T`, last `SL_*` reason) | enum | ev | `FAILED` = `DEFECT` |
| Graph numeric-tier counters (`graph_saturations[field]`, incl. `DT_CLAMP`) and the other `graph_*` counters | Graph diagnostics channel (lsg:735; LS-24) | counts per field | R | any field above 0 = `WARN` (LS-24: numeric soft invariant, "every test suite asserts `graph_saturations` is all zeros") |

| Perceived `displayed_value`, `displayed_pm` | `perceived(ref, viewer)` | the owner's unit; per mille | R | none |
| `lie_id`, `lie_kind`, `leak_modes`, `leak_amplitude_pm`, `motion_mode` | the `Perceived` record | enums; per mille | R | none |
| Ledger `lie_id`, `kind`, `direction`, `state`, `start_run`, `active_run`, `end_run` (and the `paired` mark: ledger `lie_id` equals `Perceived.lie_id`) | `sanity.ledger` | enums; runs | R | none *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `disc`, `tol_eff`, `ledger_active`, `t_ret`, `t_stall` and `t_return_max` while `RETURN`, class with its second tag and `why` | model (`desync_classifier`) | the owner's unit | R | the class severity (class table) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `lie_intent` (`d_int_u`, `short_reach`, `not_drawn`) and `lie_targets(ref)` | Filter dev channel (OQ-DO-26) | owner's unit; flags; enum | R | none; `UNVERIFIED` (why `INTENT_UNKNOWN`) when unpublished *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| Drawn value (pre-snap, millidegrees) and drawn leak | Instruments probe (P-2), optional | millidegrees; per mille | frame | `DEFECT_BYPASS`, `DEFECT_NO_LEAK`; `ABSENT` without the probe *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Defect ring, the sticky `defects seen` count (total and per class), the `unverified` count (now / seen) and the coverage readout (`checks live: intent n/N, leak-probe n/N, bypass-probe n/N`) | model (DO-35) | events; counts | ev | none (each ring event is a defect already counted; an `unverified` result is a `NOTE`, never a defect) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| The 15 Filter counters (PF-41, registry `filter_counters`): `lies_drawn`, `leak_modes_dropped`, `leak_repaired`, `lies_partial`, `lie_short_reach`, `lie_unrenderable`, `lie_collisions`, `remote_queries`, `filter_route`, `filter_pass_disabled`, `first_frame_filtered`, `tag_defaults_applied`, `filter_draws_dropped`, `lies_zone_missed`, `render_cpu_us` | Filter dev surface (PF-41) | counts; `render_cpu_us` in us; `filter_route` enum; `filter_pass_disabled` enum or bool | R | `lies_drawn` above 0 = `NOTE`. `filter_pass_disabled` = `NO_MASK` is `DEFECT` with the red mark (pipe:735, pipe:991); any other disabled reason (true) = `NOTE`; false = `OK`. `filter_route` container state `NOT_USED` = `OK` (not red, pf:446, AC-PF-38). `lies_partial`, `lie_short_reach`, `lie_unrenderable`, `lies_zone_missed`, `filter_draws_dropped` above 0 = `WARN`. `leak_repaired` above 0 = `DEFECT` (the Filter's severity, registry `filter_counters`); `lie_collisions`, `leak_modes_dropped`, `first_frame_filtered`, `tag_defaults_applied` above 0 = `NOTE`. `remote_queries` above 0 = `NOTE` in MVP (it is the leakage indicator, DO-34). `render_cpu_us` is compared as a **rolling median over `DIAG_FILTER_CPU_WINDOW_FRAMES` frames** against the registry's `CPU_BUDGET_MS` 0.5 (a median per frame, AC-PF-43b): median above 0.5 = `WARN`, never a per-frame verdict. **All counter severities are the Filter's own (registry `filter_counters`)** (OQ-DO-2 resolved 2026-10-27; every counter not named above = `NOTE`): PF-41 names the counters, not their severity. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* |

*P-SANITY* (DO-24; rows per `PlayerId`; sources `sanity.state`, `sanity_view(player_id)`, `sanity.ledger`)

| Row | Source | Unit | Cadence | Severity rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Level, `stage_dwell` | `sanity.state` | mSU; runs | R | none |
| `stage`, `tell_on`, `harm_eligible` | `sanity_view(player_id)` | enums; bool | R | none |
| Ledger `lies[]` | `sanity.ledger` (sn:170) | **every field Sanity publishes in `lies[]`**, read from the owner's schema and never from a literal copy in the Overlay: at sn:170 as of 2026-10-27 that is `{lie_id, slot, subject_ref {stable_id, surface}, kind, params, direction, state, onset_stage, start_run, active_run, end_run, debunked_run, leak_modes, first_lie}` (`active_run = start_run + delay_runs`, `end_run = active_run + life_runs`, SN-16). `direction` (COMFORT or ALARM) is shown because it is how an operator judges whether a comfort-direction lie is fair (Sanity L-2I and L-W); a field Sanity adds later appears on the row without an Overlay edit (AC-DO-59). `state` shows `PENDING`, `ACTIVE`, `EXPIRED`, `DEBUNKED`, `INVALIDATED`, and only `ACTIVE` (listed for the viewer, `ledger_listed`) is `ledger_active` (DO-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* | R | none |
| `immune[]` | `sanity.ledger` | rows | R | none |
| Telemetry counters | Sanity (SN-28): `eligible_entries_per_min`, lies shown, acted on, debunked, expired unseen | counts; per minute | R | none (Sanity owns its invariants) |
| `FORCED` tag | `sanity.state` `forced` (per-seat dev diagnostic flag, SN-28; set by `force_stage` / `force_level`, cleared only by NEW_GAME, always false in a release export) | tag | ev | read each refresh; a forced seat = `NOTE` |
| Stage-entry stopwatch | `stage_entry_latency_s` (observer `mark`) | seconds | ev | none (Sanity's AC owns the 268 to 363 s window) |

### States and Transitions

The Overlay has no simulation state and no kernel state of its own (DO-9). It has three dev-session machines: the **mode machine** (what is drawn), the **channel status machine** (what each registered channel reports), and a **behaviour overlay** keyed to the kernel's state (what the Overlay does in each of the kernel's six states). None is a block, none enters a snapshot or `state_hash`, and no transition submits a command or changes `T`.

**1. Mode machine** (DO-6 to DO-8). Who may cause a transition: only the operator, through the Input Adapter's forwarded dev actions (DO-8), or the composition root at launch (the launch option). Nothing in the simulation, the Filter, Sanity or any sibling can change the mode.

| State | Subscribed | Collecting | Draws | Entry | Exit | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `OFF` | yes (registered) | no: the handler returns at once | nothing | Dev launch default (`OVERLAY_DEFAULT_MODE`); a confirmed `toggle` from `FULL` or `OBSERVER_STRIP` | `toggle` to `FULL` | The only per-tick cost is one early-out call. Histories are cleared on entry; the defect ring, the marks and the observer log are kept (DO-35). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `FULL` | yes | yes: everything | pages `P-KERNEL` to `P-FRAME`, then `P-CHANNELS`; the world debug draws | `toggle` from `OFF`; the `reveal` chord from `OBSERVER_STRIP` (history preserved) | a confirmed `toggle` to `OFF`; `mode` to `OBSERVER_STRIP` | The tester-hostile mode (DO-7). Page changes and the selection actions (`cursor_next`, `cursor_prev`, `activate`) act only here. World debug draws render only here (DO-26). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `OBSERVER_STRIP` | yes | the observer set (DO-35) | one text line (DO-7) | `mode` from `FULL`; the `reveal` chord from `HIDDEN` | a confirmed `toggle` to `OFF`; `mode` to `HIDDEN`; the `reveal` chord to `FULL` | Shows no ref and no ledger field, but names the stage and phase, so it is tester-hostile too. Windows that the observer set does not collect (hook window, dirty peaks, channel pulls) stop advancing and are **kept, not cleared**; they resume on re-entry to `FULL`, and a window that spans the gap is labelled `gap` until it refills. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `HIDDEN` | yes | the observer set only (DO-35) | nothing | `mode` from `OBSERVER_STRIP`; the dev launch option `--overlay=hidden` (placeholder); the playtest preset (forced at launch, DO-6) | The `reveal` chord only (dev preset; the playtest preset registers no `reveal`, so a playtest session never leaves `HIDDEN`, R16) | Plain actions are ignored, except `mark`, which is accepted silently and echoed to the log. The observer sees the log, not the windows; the heavy windows start empty on reveal and say "insufficient samples (n/N)". *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `HIDDEN` no longer pulls every channel during the session it protects)* |

| From | Action | To | Condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| `OFF` | `toggle` | `FULL` | The dev predicate holds (otherwise the action does not exist) |
| `FULL`, `OBSERVER_STRIP` | `toggle` | unchanged, confirm pending | The first press shows "press toggle again within `DIAG_OFF_CONFIRM_S` to clear history" and changes nothing; a stray `toggle` can no longer destroy history *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| confirm pending | `toggle` within `DIAG_OFF_CONFIRM_S` | `OFF` | Histories cleared; the ring, the marks and the log are kept. Any other action, or the timeout, clears the prompt (the window runs on accumulated process delta, never on a wall clock) |
| `FULL` | `mode` | `OBSERVER_STRIP` | none |
| `OBSERVER_STRIP` | `mode` | `HIDDEN` | none |
| `OBSERVER_STRIP` | `reveal` chord | `FULL` | History preserved: the only route from the strip to `FULL`, so a stray `toggle` is never the way back *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `HIDDEN` | `mode`, `toggle`, `page_next`, `page_prev`, `dump`, `cursor_next`, `cursor_prev`, `activate` | unchanged (ignored) | Plain actions do nothing in `HIDDEN` so a tester cannot expose anything |
| `HIDDEN` | `mark` | unchanged | Accepted silently: nothing is drawn; a `MARK` line goes to the observer log (DO-35). In the playtest preset this is the only registered action *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R16)* |
| `HIDDEN` | `reveal` chord | `OBSERVER_STRIP` | Dev preset only: the playtest preset registers no `reveal`, so this row does not exist there *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R16)*. Reveal goes to the strip, not `FULL`; a second chord from the strip is needed to reach `FULL`, so showing a lie takes two deliberate chords |
| `FULL` | `reveal` chord | unchanged (ignored) | The chord only moves `HIDDEN` toward `FULL` |
| `FULL` | `page_next`, `page_prev` | same mode, adjacent page | Fixed page order, wraps |
| `FULL` | `cursor_next`, `cursor_prev`, `activate` | unchanged | `HIDDEN` and `OBSERVER_STRIP` ignore them |
| any visible mode | `dump` | unchanged | Writes the text snapshot (DO-31); from `OBSERVER_STRIP` it dumps every page, not just the strip |
| any | `mark` | unchanged | Stamps `T` as an observer mark; the on-screen list keeps up to `DIAG_MARK_MAX`, the log keeps every mark (DO-35) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| any | kernel enters `FAULTED` in a dev build | unchanged | Forces the FAULT record onto the strip and, in `FULL`, onto `P-KERNEL`; `HIDDEN` stays hidden (the tester must not see it either) |
| any | release export | not reachable | The root is absent (DO-2); the actions are not registered |

*(Design note: the `mode` cycle runs `FULL` to `OBSERVER_STRIP` to `HIDDEN` and stops there; `HIDDEN` leaves only by the chord, the chord leads to the strip, and a second chord from the strip leads to `FULL` with history preserved. `FULL` is entered from `OFF` by `toggle` (empty history) and from the strip by the chord (history kept); a stray `toggle` in `FULL` or the strip only raises a confirm prompt. `OFF` is distinct from `HIDDEN` because `OFF` stops collection and clears the history windows, whereas `HIDDEN` keeps collecting the observer set (the strip fields, the defect ring and the log) at the lowest cost.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**2. Channel status machine** (DO-12, DO-14). Who may cause a transition: the owner's registration (at composition-root time), the owner's `pull()` results, and the Overlay's own fault isolation. The Overlay shows the status on `P-CHANNELS`.

| Status | Entry | Exit | Meaning and display |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PENDING` | The channel is in `expected_channels` and not yet registered, **while the kernel is `BOOTING`** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | A successful registration (still inside `BOOTING`) and first `pull()`; or the end of `BOOTING`, when an unregistered expected channel becomes `NOTE absent` (a registration attempted later is `REJECTED`, never a late `PENDING` exit) | Shown as `PENDING`, severity `NOTE`; the page does not wait |
| `OK` | A successful `pull()` within `DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S` | A `pull()` error; no update for `DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S` | The rows are evaluated by their declared `severity_rule` |
| `STALE` | No update for `DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S` | A successful `pull()` | Shown as `STALE`, severity `NOTE`; the last values stay visible, marked |
| `ERROR` | A `pull()` returns an invalid result, or a registration or page render receives invalid data (`DIAG_ERR`, DO-14) | A successful `pull()`; or `SUSPENDED` after `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` consecutive failures | One `DEFECT` row names the `channel_id` and the `DIAG_ERR` value (the kernel's fault channel, CR-19, is the precedent: validated results and a completion sentinel, no exceptions; this is the inner layer) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `SUSPENDED` | `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` consecutive failures | The next mode change | Not pulled; the row stays a `DEFECT` so the failure cannot go unnoticed |
| `REJECTED` | A registration error: duplicate `channel_id`, `DIAG_CHANNELS_MAX` or `DIAG_ROWS_PER_CHANNEL_MAX` exceeded | A corrected registration at the next launch | A registration error row, never a crash |

**3. Behaviour per kernel state** (DO-11). The kernel's six states are the kernel's (kern:151); the table says what the Overlay does in each. Nothing the Overlay does changes a kernel state.

| Kernel state | Model input | Draws | Classifier | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `BOOTING` | Boot progress; on failure the unknown `hook_id` or the cycle path (kern:606); no sim read views yet | The boot-progress row; on failure the failure row | `UNAVAILABLE` / `NOT_RUNNING` | A registration attempted after `BOOTING` is `REJECTED` (`LATE_REGISTRATION`) and never appears *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Channel registration is accepted only while the kernel is `BOOTING`, a rule this GDD adopts by analogy with Kernel CR-6, which itself covers blocks, command types and hooks only, so a channel that misses it never appears. |
| `LOADING` | Kernel progress only; sim read views are not read | A `LOADING` banner over the frozen last page | `UNAVAILABLE` / `NOT_RUNNING` | Histories are cleared at the exit edge (DO-32) |
| `RUNNING` | All views and diagnostics | All pages | Live | The reference state |
| `PAUSED` | Frozen `T`; views stable | All pages; numerals still refresh | Live (values stable) | The kernel runs a paused service cycle (P1, P2, P4, P5, P6 with no P3, `T` unchanged, CR-16) for non-diegetic commands, so P5 batches can still arrive with `T` unchanged: the Overlay counts them but folds no hook timing. The kernel forces a hash on pause (DO-33). |
| `SAVING` | All; `save_status` `WRITING` / `WRITTEN` | All pages plus the Save/Load channel's save row | Live | The save row is the Save/Load channel's (`save_status`, last generation, `written_at_T`; sl:405-407) |
| `FAULTED` (dev build) | The FAULT record `(T, type, issuer, host_order, block_id, assertion)` and the last good views | The FAULT record pinned; other pages frozen | `UNAVAILABLE` / `NOT_RUNNING` | The kernel is not ticking; the render loop and the Overlay keep running (chart-table-web-map-and-menus.md:377 accepted as written). In a non-dev, non-playtest build the kernel returns to the menu and the Overlay does not exist; in the playtest preset the Overlay exists in `HIDDEN`, draws nothing, and the sink writes the FAULT line and flushes at once (DO-35) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. |

**4. Per-reference classifier state** (DO-21). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: tick-denominated.)* A reference's class is recomputed at each **class fold** (DO-21 (5): at most once per change of Sanity's `run`, independent of the numeral refresh, the throttle, the rendered frame rate and `Engine.time_scale`) and has no memory except counters in **kernel ticks** that the model keeps per `(ref, viewer)`: `n_eq` (ticks, at class folds, on which `disc <= tol_eff`, the Filter intended a visible departure and `lie_age >= reach_age`; 0 until the age gate opens), `n_skew` (ticks the ledger and the `Perceived` record disagree on a lie's presence, `ledger_active` XOR `lie_id != 0` while `motion_mode != RETURN`, with `not_drawn` NONE), `n_leak` (ticks `drawn_leak(ref)` is 0 against an amplitude at or above `floor_eff` (the stage-indexed `AMP_FLOOR_PM`, 400/325/250)) and the return trackers `t_ret`, `T_stall` and the progress window, plus values it latches from the owners rather than counts: the lie age `run - active_run` read off the ledger row, and `disc_end` / `tv_end` captured on the first frame of `RETURN` (the envelope's anchor). A counter advances by `dT` only when `T` has changed since the previous fold, so it **freezes while the kernel is `PAUSED`** however many rendered frames pass: a paused session cannot accrue silent-lie time. They reset on a restore (DO-32), on a viewer switch, on a mode change from `OFF`, and when a lie ends. Typical class progressions, each a sequence of classes over class folds, are: *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*

| Situation | Progression |
|---|---|
| A fair lie begins and ends | `HONEST` -> `SKEW` (at most `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS` 24 ticks, two class folds; Sanity's own `PENDING` row is not `ledger_active`, so the 2 to 5 s onset delay shows as `HONEST`) -> `DELIBERATE` (`ONSET` while the lie age is below the reach age or `disc` is still inside `tol_eff`) -> `DELIBERATE` -> (entry removed) [`SKEW` for the end-of-lie gap while the Filter's `lie_id` is still non-zero, at most `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS`, absent when the Filter's `RETURN` starts on the same fold] -> `RETURNING` -> `HONEST` (a needle lie with room for a return; a binary-frame, ineligible-needle or short-reach lie goes `DELIBERATE` -> [`SKEW`] -> `HONEST` at removal and never shows `RETURNING`, DO-21 (1), AC-DO-36, AC-DO-155, AC-DO-195). A seat that leaves the lie's compartment changes nothing here: the row stays `ACTIVE` and listed (`away`), DO-21, E-DO-53 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| A lie that never renders although the Filter intended a visible departure | `HONEST` -> `SKEW` -> `DELIBERATE` (`ONSET`) -> `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` once the lie age reaches `ceil(GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x (end_run - active_run))` runs **and** `n_eq` passes `DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS` ticks |
| A lie the Filter designed with no needle departure (no room, an intended departure inside the tolerance, an ineligible needle, a true value sweeping through an absolute target) | `HONEST` -> `SKEW` -> `DELIBERATE` tagged `SHORT_REACH`, `SUB_TOL` or `NO_NEEDLE`, never `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| A stuck return | `RETURNING` -> `DEFECT_UNCAUSED` (`RETURN_STALLED`) when `t_stall` passes `t_return_max(lie)` or the windowed progress floor fails, or (`RETURN_GROWTH`) when the discrepancy grows past the envelope or the step bound *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| A long honest return on a degrading node | `RETURNING` tagged `TARGET_MOVING` for the whole return, however long it outlasts `t_return_max(lie)` (the stall clock is re-anchored by every fold on which the follower moved), then `HONEST` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| A restore with the Overlay open | any -> `UNAVAILABLE` / `SETTLING` for `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` ticks -> live |

### Interactions with Other Systems

**The Overlay registers no hook, owns no block, exposes no mutator, no command type, no `read_view` and no dirty key of its own, so it fixes no interface name in any registry.** Every interface below belongs to the other side; "data in" is what the Overlay reads, "data out" is what it gives back (a channel registration, a debug-draw toggle, a request) and never a write to the simulation. There are **no downstream dependents among the systems designed in this run**: the roughly seventeen siblings that name the Overlay are the *owners* of what it reads, and the tables below are the Overlay's bidirectional row for each of them. Disk status was checked with Glob on `design/gdd/` at authoring and re-checked at the 2026-10-27 re-review (28 files: 26 system GDDs including this one, plus the systems index and the concept) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: every system named below as "designed" is on disk; HUD & Suit Layer is designed (Status Designed, on disk); In-World Instruments & Inspect View is designed (this run) (Status Designed, on disk); Accessibility Settings, Settings & Input Rebinding, Onboarding, Co-op Session & Replication, Crew Strip, Chat/Voice/Pings, the dev console and every ADR have no file.

**Freshness** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: every neighbour claim below was re-verified against the current files on 2026-10-27. APPLIED since authoring: the Graph's downstream row (life-support-graph.md:283, :882), Suit's callers table and Dependencies row (suit-and-equipment.md:307, :989) with the `suit_*` diagnostics frozen (SE-40), the Controller's Interactions row and dev-only note registering the ten actions with the L3 + R3 pad chord (player-controller-input.md:102, :277, :827), the Filter's flipped rows (perception-filter.md:552, :1157) and its published `t_return_max`, calibration and counter severities, and the kernel's `diagnostics` accessor with `hash_T` and `hash_algo`, `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS`, the fault channel CR-19 and the tick driver CR-1a (command-simulation-layer.md). Statuses below are flipped accordingly. AC-DO-71 and AC-DO-115 leave `[blocked]` only after a green run against the real Adapter, not on the amendment alone.

## Formulas

**Scope and determinism.** The Overlay defines **nine** formula blocks (the ruling's eight formulas, with `graph_hook_tick` and `hook_window_p99` registered separately), and all nine are display arithmetic: none is hashed, replicated, saved or read by a simulation system, and none can change `state_hash` (DO-9). Where the inputs are integers (every Graph, Suit and kernel quantity is an integer unit, `mC`, `mGU`, `dkPa`, `permille`, `bp`, ticks, runs, microseconds) the arithmetic is integer, with explicit floor and ceiling, so the table-driven tests of the classifier and the percentile are exact and identical on every machine. Where a locked formula belongs to another GDD the Overlay **cites it by name and never re-expresses it with other numbers**: `step_over_budget` and `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 ("a threshold, not a cap", kern D.6), `state_hash` (kern D.12), `log_coverage_ticks` (kern D.13, `COMMAND_LOG_CAPACITY` 1024), `lag_ticks` (kern:351, repeated only in the form the kernel publishes it), and the Filter's return path (pf D.6). Every `DIAG_*` / `OVERLAY_*` value below is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` with a range in Tuning Knobs. Proposed registry names for the merge stage (written from this final text, never from a draft): `desync_classifier`, `graph_hook_tick`, `hook_window_p99`, `dirty_load_pm`, `queue_load_pm`, `ticks_per_overlay_refresh`, `overlay_cpu_us`, `hash_cost_per_s`, `stage_entry_latency_s`. The nine blocks below are the ruling's eight formulas (R30's six, plus `queue_load_pm`, plus `stage_entry_latency_s`), with the pair "`graph_hook_tick` and `hook_window_p99`" written as two blocks (F-2, F-3) because the registry names them separately. **Determinism across frame rates** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the classifier and every counter are functions of kernel ticks and of the class folds (DO-21 (5)), never of the rendered frame rate, the refresh throttle or `Engine.time_scale`; the same `T` gives the same class at 20, 60 and 144 fps, within one fold interval (12 ticks) or exactly in tick-driven mode (AC-DO-156).

### F-1. `desync_classifier`

The `desync_classifier` formula is defined as:

`class(ref, viewer) = first_match(row_1 ... row_9 of the DO-21 class table)`, evaluated at each class fold (DO-21 (5): at most once per Sanity run, independent of `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`) from the fold's own same-frame read of `(tv, p, ledger row, lie_intent, Filter counters)`; the numeral refresh only displays the latest fold *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

The sub-expressions the rows use (all integers in the **owner's unit** of the ref: `mC`, `mGU`, `dkPa`, `permille`, `bp`; binary-frame refs compare as 0 or 1) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: `sanity_listed`, `live_row`, `subject_rows`, `scope`, `ledger_active`, `dT` and `n_eq`; `dT` also delegated ruling R13)*:

```
binary_frame(ref) : if true, span, arc_deg and q_display are NOT consulted (a binary-frame ref has no PF-45 dial calibration): q_display is unset, tol_eff = 0, p_int = the displayed frame state (0 or 1), disc = 0 if p_int == tv else 1, step_enabled = false, and no check below returns UNAVAILABLE / NO_RESOLVER for a missing span or arc. binary_frame is true for STATE_GLYPH, HOUSING_GLOW, MACHINE_FRAME and any ref carrying a SPRITE_SWAP lie (DO-21)
span          = value_hi - value_lo                             # needle and readout refs only (skipped when binary_frame(ref)); > 0 and arc_deg > 0, else UNAVAILABLE / NO_RESOLVER; from the PF-45 calibration
floor_div(a,b)= a div b rounded toward minus infinity           # GDScript int '/' truncates toward zero: use floori(float(a) / b) or a sign-corrected form
p_int         = displayed frame state (0 or 1)                  if binary_frame(ref)            # no span, arc or q_display is read
              = p.displayed_value                               if p.motion_mode == HONEST      # honest path: exact and an integer by the Filter contract, never rounded
              = floor_div(value_lo * 1000 + span * p.displayed_pm + 500, 1000)   otherwise      # integer arithmetic from the integer displayed_pm; half rounds up, for negative values too
disc          = abs(p_int - tv)                                 # binary-frame refs: 0 if equal else 1
q_display     = ceil_div(span, 1000)                            # owner's units, >= 1; from the PF-45 calibration (published by the Filter, registry `filter_counters`); unset when binary_frame(ref)
tol_eff       = max(tol(kind), q_display)                       # if q_display is unpublished or binary_frame(ref): tol_eff = tol(kind), which is 0 for a binary-frame ref
step_enabled  = (q_display is published)                        # a RETURN exists only for a calibrated ref (PF-44), so this is true whenever it matters
target        = lie_targets(ref)                                # published by the Filter (the PF-9 bijection ref <-> subject_ref {stable_id, surface}, plus the ref's quantity), or NONE
sanity_listed(row) = ledger_listed(viewer, row.lie_id)             # Sanity's DIAGNOSTIC accessor (SN-23): true iff the row is in sanity.perception for the seat this run, i.e. the row is ACTIVE, in the seat's compartment or `away` (every ACTIVE row is listed, SN-23 as amended 2026-10-28); the Overlay never reads sanity.perception. This is the ONLY use of lie_id in the join (membership); the match itself is still by (viewer, subject_ref, surface/quantity), never by lie_id
live_row(row)      = row.viewer == viewer and row.state == ACTIVE and sanity_listed(row)   # an ACTIVE row is live whether or not the seat is in its compartment (`away`, EC-SN-35); a listing that disagrees with ACTIVE is never guessed (it would only arise from a Sanity defect)
subject_rows(ref)  = { r : live_row(r) and r.subject_ref.stable_id == the stable_id of the ref's PF-9 subject }   # needs the ref-to-subject mapping only, not lie_targets
scope         = NONE            if the ref has no ref-to-subject mapping (PF-9)                                                                    # UNAVAILABLE / NO_RESOLVER
              = NONE            if ledger_listed is unpublished and the subject has >= 1 row in state ACTIVE for the viewer                       # UNAVAILABLE / NO_RESOLVER (the listing is unknown, never a guess)
              = RESOLVED        if |subject_rows(ref)| == 0                                                                                       # vacuous join: no live row on the subject, so ledger_active is false for EVERY ref of it whatever lie_targets says; this is the end-of-lie case (the ledger row ended, the Filter's lie_id lags one run)
              = RESOLVED        if target is published, or the ref's subject has exactly one ref
              = SUBJECT_SCOPE   if target is unpublished or ambiguous, the subject has more than one ref and |subject_rows(ref)| == 1           # the one degradation rule (E-DO-49 and AC-DO-163 cite it)
              = NONE            if target is unpublished or ambiguous, the subject has more than one ref and |subject_rows(ref)| >= 2              # UNAVAILABLE / NO_RESOLVER, never a guess
              (evaluated in this order, first match wins)
ledger_active = (scope == RESOLVED and exists r in subject_rows(ref) with r.subject_ref == target's {stable_id, surface} (and the quantity where the PF-9 triple carries one; a single-ref subject matches on stable_id))
                or scope == SUBJECT_SCOPE                           # lowest (start_run, lie_id) wins among matches (PF-46); SUBJECT_SCOPE is subject granularity: true for EVERY ref of the subject, none asserted verified
unverified    = scope == SUBJECT_SCOPE or not intent_published      # row 6 then shows the tag UNVERIFIED (why: SUBJECT_SCOPE or INTENT_UNKNOWN), never LIE
fold          = at most one per change of run (T crosses a multiple of SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR = 12); independent of DIAG_REFRESH_HZ, the throttle, fps and Engine.time_scale
dT            = min(T_fold - T_prev_fold, SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR + MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME - 1)   # ticks: 12 normally, up to 15 on a catch-up frame; 0 means no fold and no counter moves (PAUSED). On the FIRST fold of a session and on a restore (a LOADING -> RUNNING edge since the previous fold (DO-32), or T < T_prev_fold, or a change of kernel.clock restore_epoch): dT = 0 and every counter and anchor clears. The LOADING edge is the signal that never misses: a Continue of the newest QUIT generation carries restore_epoch unchanged and restores a T that need not be below T_prev_fold (delegated ruling R1 as amended by R13/R14), so the epoch is corroboration only, neither sufficient nor necessary (Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R13; cross-GDD fix)
lie_age       = run - active_run                            # runs since activation; Sanity runs at TICK_RATE / SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR = 60 / 12 = 5 per s
reach_age     = ceil_div(GLIDE_REACH_FRAC_pm * (end_run - active_run), 1000)    # GLIDE_REACH_FRAC 0.6 -> 600 pm; binary-frame refs: SWAP_LEAD_S * 5; +infinity when end_run <= active_run (an unset end_run never reaches the gate). A lie shorter than reach_age + DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS cannot be assessed and reads ONSET for its life (Sanity's minimum life is 200 runs, so this is out of contract)
onset         = silent_ok and (lie_age < reach_age or (disc <= tol_eff and n_eq <= DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS))   # the exact ONSET predicate (AC-DO-24 to AC-DO-30 agree): the departure has not yet had its age, or it has and is still inside the grace
d_int_u       = floor_div(lie_intent.d_intended_pm * span + 500, 1000)           # the Filter's intended departure in the owner's unit; binary-frame refs: 1 if not_drawn == NONE else 0
silent_ok     = intent_published and lie_intent.not_drawn == NONE and not lie_intent.short_reach and d_int_u > tol_eff
n_eq          = n_eq_prev   + dT  if (scope == RESOLVED and ledger_active and lie_age >= reach_age and disc <= tol_eff and silent_ok) else 0         # ticks, at a fold; 0 whenever no live row matches (the lie_age / reach_age inputs exist only while the row is ACTIVE)
n_skew        = n_skew_prev + dT  if (scope == RESOLVED and (ledger_active XOR (lie_id != 0 and motion_mode != RETURN)) and lie_intent.not_drawn == NONE) else 0   # ticks
n_diff        = n_diff_prev + 1   if (lie_id == 0 and not ledger_active and motion_mode != RETURN and p_int != tv) else 0     # FOLDS, not ticks; see row_9
n_leak        = n_leak_prev + dT  if (lie_id != 0 and motion_mode != RETURN and leak_probe_present and drawn_leak(ref) == 0 and p.leak_amplitude_pm >= floor_eff)   # floor_eff = stage-indexed AMP_FLOOR_PM, 400/325/250 at stages 0-1/2/3 else 0   # ticks
t_ret         = (T - T_ret_start) / TICK_RATE  seconds         # ticks, not frames: freezes when the kernel is PAUSED
progress      = sign(tv - p_int_prev) * (p_int - p_int_prev)    # the displayed value moved toward the true value this fold (owner's unit; negative = away)
T_stall       = T_fold if progress >= q_display else T_stall    # starts at T_ret_start; re-anchored while the follower is still following
t_stall       = (T - T_stall) / TICK_RATE                       # seconds without progress, ticks-based
closing(t)    = if binary_frame(ref):               disc == 0
                elif t_ret <  WANDER_BLEND_S:       disc_t <= disc_end + abs(tv_t - tv_end) + tol_eff               # envelope
                elif step_enabled:                  disc_t <= disc_prev + abs(tv_t - tv_prev) + q_display          # step test at fold cadence; slack is the display quantum
                                                    and ( t_ret < WANDER_BLEND_S + DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS / TICK_RATE
                                                          or sum(progress over the last DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS) >= min(DIAG_RETURN_FLOOR_QD * q_display, disc_win) )   # windowed progress floor; disc_win = disc at the window start
                else:                               true                                                            # held by the stall bound only
row_1  UNAVAILABLE        if any input is missing (reasons: NOT_RUNNING, SETTLING, REMOTE_VIEWER, NO_LEDGER, NO_RESOLVER, NO_TRUE_SOURCE, and the row_9 case MEMO_UNCONFIRMED)
row_2  SKEW               if scope == RESOLVED and n_skew >= 1 and n_skew <= DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS
row_3  DEFECT_NO_LEAK     if lie_id != 0 and motion_mode != RETURN and ( (leak_modes & GLEAM) == 0                  # tripwire on PF-15
                                                                         or (d_leak_repaired > 0 and lie_id == leak_repaired_lie_id)   # only when the Filter publishes the repaired lie's id (OQ-DO-26 (A)); leak_repaired is a GLOBAL counter, so otherwise one page-level UNATTRIBUTED event per counter edge, never a per-ref class
                                                                         or n_leak > DIAG_NO_LEAK_GRACE_TICKS )      # the gleam was asked for and never drawn (probe)
row_4  DEFECT_SILENT_LIE  if scope == RESOLVED and ledger_active and intent_published and ( (lie_id != 0 and n_eq > DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS)
                                                                      or (lie_id == 0 and lie_intent.not_drawn == NONE and n_skew > DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS) )
row_5  DEFECT_BYPASS      if probe_present and abs(drawn_mdeg - angle_mdeg(p.displayed_pm)) > LEAK_EPS_MDEG   # integer millidegrees, pre-snap, inclusive
row_6  DELIBERATE         if ledger_active and ( (lie_id != 0 and (leak_modes & GLEAM) != 0) or (lie_id == 0 and (lie_intent.not_drawn != NONE or not intent_published)) or scope == SUBJECT_SCOPE )   # (not row_4); second tag NO_NEEDLE > SHORT_REACH > SUB_TOL > ONSET (onset predicate above); the DISPLAY tag is LIE only when not unverified, and UNVERIFIED (why SUBJECT_SCOPE or INTENT_UNKNOWN) replaces LIE when unverified: missing verification is never mapped to the verified class
row_7  RETURNING          if motion_mode == RETURN and not ledger_active and disc != 0 and t_stall <= t_return_max(lie) and closing(t)
row_8  HONEST             if not ledger_active and (lie_id == 0 or motion_mode == RETURN) and p_int == tv            # exact equality of integers, never tol; a closed return (p_int == tv) is HONEST
row_9  DEFECT_UNCAUSED    otherwise (with a why sub-tag); a result whose why is NO_LEDGER_DIFF is shown as UNAVAILABLE / MEMO_UNCONFIRMED until n_diff >= DIAG_NO_LEDGER_DIFF_FOLDS (2), because on a fold frame the Overlay's perceived() call may be the first evaluation, not a memo hit, and a memo filled before the tick notification would give a one-frame false defect (OQ-DO-28)                                                              # the catch-all: no input falls through
angle_mdeg(pm)= floor_div((pm - 500) * arc_mdeg, 1000)         # arc_mdeg = round_half_up(arc_deg * 1000); the needle angle a per-mille value maps to (IW theta(pm)), in integer millidegrees
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tv` | int, owner's unit | the owner's range (Graph `hp` 0 to 10000 bp; compartment `T` in mC; `P` in dkPa) | Graph `true_value(kind, id)` or Suit `true_value(key, player_id)` | The authoritative value, read first in the refresh (DO-20) |
| `p.displayed_value` | owner's unit (derived from integer `displayed_pm` 0..1000) | the instrument range | `perceived(ref, viewer)` (PF-7, PF-8), a hit on the Filter's per-frame memo | The perceived value; equals `tv` exactly on the honest path (pf:247) |
| `lie_id`, `leak_modes`, `motion_mode` | int, bitmask {GLEAM 1, GLIDE 2, SOUND 4}, enum {HONEST, GLIDE, TICK, STALE, BREATH, RETURN} (BREATH, the static-pair gleam breath, added to PF-8 by the Filter 2026-10-27; the classifier treats it like any lying mode) | per PF-8 | the `Perceived` record | The Filter's report of whether it drew a lie and how it leaks |
| `ledger_active` | bool | true / false | `sanity.ledger` (HOST_ONLY), joined by `(viewer, subject_ref {stable_id, surface})` through the published `lie_targets(ref)` and never by `lie_id` | Only a live row (state `ACTIVE` and listed for the viewer, `ledger_listed`) that matches the ref's own `subject_ref`; in `SUBJECT_SCOPE` it is true at subject granularity for every ref of the subject; Sanity `PENDING` and the ended states are not active (DO-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `sanity_listed(row)`, `live_row(row)`, `subject_rows(ref)` | bool; bool; set | true / false; the live rows of the ref's subject | **Sanity's published diagnostic accessor `ledger_listed(player_id, lie_id)` (SN-23, AC-SN-180)**, joined with the ledger row's `state`; the ref-to-subject mapping (PF-9) | `sanity_listed`: the row is in `sanity.perception` for the seat this run (every `ACTIVE` row is, `away` or not; the Overlay never reads `sanity.perception`); it is the only use of `lie_id` in the join (membership). `live_row`: `ACTIVE` and listed. `subject_rows`: the live rows of the viewer on the ref's subject, needing the mapping only, not `lie_targets` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `target` = `lie_targets(ref)` | record or NONE | `{subject_ref {stable_id, surface}, quantity}` | **Requested of the Filter (the PF-9 bijection, AC-PF-112); a pre-epic dependency, OQ-DO-26 (A).** Sanity's closed `params` names no surface or quantity (sn:345), so the join rides on `subject_ref` and OQ-DO-26 (B) asks that it distinguish the quantity | Scopes the join to one ref. Unpublished or ambiguous: `scope` degrades by the one rule (`SUBJECT_SCOPE` or `UNAVAILABLE` / `NO_RESOLVER`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `scope` | enum | `RESOLVED`, `SUBJECT_SCOPE`, `NONE` | model | The one degradation rule of F-1 (DO-21 (5)(iii)): `RESOLVED` includes the vacuous case of zero live rows on the subject (`ledger_active` false for every ref, the end-of-lie gap is `SKEW`); `SUBJECT_SCOPE` classifies at subject granularity, asserts no sibling `HONEST` and verifies nothing; `NONE` is `UNAVAILABLE` / `NO_RESOLVER` (no mapping, `ledger_listed` unpublished with an `ACTIVE` row on the subject, or two or more live rows with `lie_targets` unpublished) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `binary_frame(ref)` | bool | true / false | model | True for `STATE_GLYPH`, `HOUSING_GLOW`, `MACHINE_FRAME` and any ref carrying a `SPRITE_SWAP` lie: `span`, `arc_deg` and `q_display` are skipped, `tol_eff` = 0, `p_int` is the displayed frame state (0 or 1), `disc` is 0 or 1 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `unverified`, `onset` | bool | true / false | model | `unverified`: `SUBJECT_SCOPE` or `lie_intent` unpublished, so row 6 shows `UNVERIFIED` instead of `LIE`; `onset`: the exact `ONSET` second-tag predicate *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `lie_intent` | record | `{d_intended_pm 0..1000, short_reach bool, not_drawn NONE or UNRENDERABLE or INELIGIBLE}` | **Requested of the Filter on its dev channel (D.2, D.3, E-PF-10 to E-PF-12, E-PF-56); OQ-DO-26** | The Filter's own intent per `(ref, viewer)`: how far it means to depart, whether the dial lacks room, whether a needle lie exists at all. Unpublished: row 4 is suspended, `not_drawn` reads NONE for `n_skew` only, and row 6 shows `UNVERIFIED` (why `INTENT_UNKNOWN`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `d_int_u` | int, owner's unit | 0 to the range | model | `floor_div(d_intended_pm x span + 500, 1000)`; binary-frame refs 1 if drawn else 0 |
| `silent_ok` | bool | true / false | model | The Filter intended a visible departure and drew none is possible: `lie_intent` published, `not_drawn` NONE, `short_reach` false, `d_int_u > tol_eff` |
| fold, `dT` | int ticks | 0 or at least 12 | model | A fold is at most one per change of `run`; `dT` is the ticks since the previous fold; 0 means no counter moves; a restore (a `LOADING` -> `RUNNING` edge, `T` moving backwards, or a `restore_epoch` change as corroboration) gives `dT` 0 at the first post-restore fold and clears every counter and anchor *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R13)* |
