It has **no** scripting, core, export or debugging file (this domain has none): export presets and resource filters, feature tags, `OS.has_feature`, `OS.is_debug_build`, the `Performance` singleton, `Viewport.get_render_info`, `EngineDebugger` and multi-window `Window` are all `[UNV, HIGH]`; `ResourceLoader.exists`, `InputEvent.is_match`, `Callable.is_valid` and `Node.process_priority`, used by DO-2, DO-8, DO-14 and DO-5, are likewise absent from the reference and `[UNV]` for 4.7.2 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Six spikes: **S-1** export file list (release-export an empty scene plus one file under the dev root, list the files, and boot the release export with the dev root absent); **S-2** feature-tag registration at the composition root (`OS.has_feature` in editor, dev export, playtest export and release export, including that the custom tags read false in an editor run and that the kernel `diagnostics` accessor exists in the release template); **S-3** an optional second window for the observer (not required for MVP); **S-4** world debug-draw colour measured unchanged under `force_stage` 3 (DO-26); **S-5** `Viewport.get_render_info` and `Performance` per-viewport draw-count fields on 4.7.2 (AC-DO-87; fallback: `P-FRAME` shows `n/a`); **S-6** the measured cost and worst-frame spike of the GDScript `state_hash` over 100 to 300 KiB (AC-DO-181; with Save/Load OQ-17). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**F. ADR candidates touching this system** (none exist; `docs/architecture/` holds only `tr-registry.yaml`): debug-gate / build-flag / export-filter (new, raised here; it also records the playtest preset of DO-6, the load discipline of DO-2 and the template choice); the diagnostics pipeline and export format (AT-32); the authority-model ADR (the tick-source half of kernel OQ 2 is closed by CR-1a, 2026-10-27); the H64 and K-1 hash ADR (sl:1316); the leak-transport ADR (pf:1848).

## Tuning Knobs

Every knob below is a **dev-session presentation knob** invented by this GDD: each is `[PLACEHOLDER]` (default, range and proposed registry name, final nowhere until a stage sets it), each is named `DIAG_*` or `OVERLAY_*`, and **none affects the simulation, a block, a snapshot or `state_hash`** (DO-9). The one exception is ★ `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS`, which is a **kernel knob (accepted 2026-10-27, command-simulation-layer.md Tuning Knobs)**: the kernel owner owns its value, its range and its home, and the Overlay depends on no value of it (DO-33). A ★ marks a cross-system fact that the registry-merge stage should hold; everything else is local to this tool and is registered only if the merge stage chooses to track dev knobs. Nothing here is exposed to a player (DO-30). The reflexion rule applies to every row: the value is a placeholder, never a fact.

| Knob (proposed registry name) | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` | 10 | Hz | 2 to 30 | Numerals unreadable, model cost up (`overlay_cpu_us`) | Laggy digits; single events missed unless peak-held (DO-18) | `ticks_per_overlay_refresh`, `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US`, peak-hold |
| ★ `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` (**kernel knob (accepted 2026-10-27, command-simulation-layer.md Tuning Knobs)**; source of truth: Command & Simulation Layer) | 60 | ticks | 6 to 600 | Drift found late (10 s at 600) | Hash cost dominates dev ticks (90 ms per real second at 1; F-8) | `hash_cost_per_s`, P-STATE age rule, kern:915 wording |
| `DIAG_HOOK_WINDOW_RUNS` | 600 | Graph hook-tick samples (200 steps = 20 s) | 100 to 3600 | A regression is diluted in the window | P99 noisy (a P99 over 100 samples is a single outlier) | `hook_window_p99`, `DIAG_P99_MIN_SAMPLES` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `DIAG_P99_MIN_SAMPLES` | 100 | hook-tick samples | 100 to 600 (at most `DIAG_HOOK_WINDOW_RUNS`; asserted at startup and clamped) | Long wait for a first verdict | A false P99 over a handful of samples | `hook_window_p99` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `DIAG_DIRTY_WARN_PM` | 500 | per mille of `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256 (500 = 128 keys) | 250 to 900 | A collapse arrives unwarned | `WARN` noise on ordinary cascades (the Graph's MVP worst is 148) | `dirty_load_pm`, `DIAG_DIRTY_ALERT_PM` |
| `DIAG_DIRTY_ALERT_PM` | 750 | per mille (192 keys) | `DIAG_DIRTY_WARN_PM` to 1000 | No pin before a collapse | Pin noise | `dirty_load_pm` (still `WARN`; no new severity) |
| `DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS` | 120 | kernel ticks (2 s), advanced only when `T` changed | 30 to 600 (at least one fold, 12) | A real silent lie is unflagged for many seconds past its reach age | False `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` while a repair-converged lie is still `ACTIVE` (up to one Sanity run, 12 ticks) or while rounding jitter sits at the boundary | `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` (Filter), `tol_eff`, `n_eq`, DO-21 row 4 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS` | 24 | kernel ticks (two Sanity runs), advanced only when `T` changed | 12 to 120 | A Filter that never picked up an entry hides as `SKEW` | `SKEW` never shown; false `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE` branch (b) on the run gap (a grace in rendered frames was shorter than one Sanity run at 144 fps) | Sanity run rate (5 per s), `n_skew`, DO-21 rows 2 and 4 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` | 12 | kernel ticks (one Sanity run; was 6 frames) | 6 to 120 | The classifier is blind after every restore; a pause inside the window holds `SETTLING` until the ticks elapse | Restore tails (E-PF-5) misread as `DEFECT_UNCAUSED`; below one fold interval the state may never be observed | DO-32, the class fold (12 ticks) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `DIAG_NO_LEAK_GRACE_TICKS` | 24 | kernel ticks | 12 to 120 | A dropped gleam stays unflagged | A false defect while the Filter's own fade-in crosses `floor_eff` | DO-21 row 3 (iii), `n_leak` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*
| `DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS` | 300 | kernel ticks (5 s) | 120 to 900 | A stalled return is flagged late | Slow honest returns near closure read as stalled | `closing(t)`, `DIAG_RETURN_FLOOR_QD` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `DIAG_RETURN_FLOOR_QD` | 5 | display quanta (`q_display`) | 2 to 20 | A slow honest follower is flagged | A creeping stall is unflagged | `closing(t)`, `q_display`, `DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |

**Count: 49 knob rows** (the floor is 30; `DIAG_NO_LEDGER_DIFF_FOLDS` and `DIAG_FOLD_REFS_MAX` were added at the 2026-10-27 re-review (the classified set is separate from the displayed list, DO-20); 13 rows were added at the 2026-10-27 design review: `DIAG_NO_LEAK_GRACE_TICKS`, `DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS`, `DIAG_RETURN_FLOOR_QD`, `DIAG_REFRESH_LADDER_HZ`, `OVERLAY_FRAME_CEILING_US`, `DIAG_OFF_CONFIRM_S`, `DIAG_DEFECT_RING_MAX`, `DIAG_LOG_MAX_LINES`, `DIAG_LOG_MAX_FILES`, `OVERLAY_LOG_STDOUT`, `DIAG_HOOK_MAX_BREACH_RUNS`, `DIAG_DEV_TEMPLATE_SCALE_PM`, `OVERLAY_PLAYTEST_TAG`; three were renamed and re-denominated in kernel ticks, the first two keeping their numbers and the settle window going from 6 frames to 12 ticks so a 12-tick fold can observe it). Rows for `DIAG_DUMP_DIR`, `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR`, `OVERLAY_LAUNCH_OPTION`, the key rows, `DIAG_REVEAL_CHORD`, `DIAG_FILTER_COUNTER_SEVERITY` and `DIAG_STRIP_FIELDS` are configuration rather than tuning. Differences from the specialist proposal, each an orchestrator ruling: `DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS` (first named `..._FRAMES`) default 240 to 120 and range to 30 to 600, because the reach-age gate (DO-21 row 4) now absorbs the slow-onset case the old default was sized for and the grace covers only rounding, wander and a one-run invalidation latency; `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS` (first named `..._FRAMES`) default 12 to 24, so the allowance spans two Sanity runs (a run is 12 ticks) instead of exactly one; `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` (first named `..._FRAMES`) default 2 to 6 and then, at design review, 12 ticks, so the settle window spans at least one class fold and `SETTLING` can actually be observed; `DIAG_FILTER_CPU_WINDOW_FRAMES` added by CP-9.

**Derived or cited values the Overlay does not own (point at the source of truth; never copy):** `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC`, `WANDER_BLEND_S`, `SWAP_LEAD_S`, `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`, `D_VIS_MIN_DEG`, `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC`, `RM_GLIDE_SCALE` and `t_return_max(lie)` belong to the Perception Filter (pf D.2, D.6, Tuning Knobs); `LEAK_EPS_DEG` 0.25 and `LEAK_FADE_S` 1.5 are the registry's `lie_leak_amplitude`; the per-field quanta (`T` 1000 mC, `P` 10 dkPa, `p_o2` 5 dkPa, store 20 permille, intensity 100 permille, `hp` 250 bp) belong to the Graph (LS-20, lsg:201) and the Suit vitals buckets to the Suit (suit-and-equipment.md:270-277 (SE-38)); `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256, `QUEUE_CAPACITY` 1024, `PER_ISSUER_CAPACITY` 256, `MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME` 4, `COMMAND_LOG_CAPACITY` 1024, `TICK_RATE` 60, `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 (a threshold, not a cap) belong to the kernel (registry entries); `GRAPH_HOOK_BUDGET_MS` (0.5 P99 / 1.0 max per hook tick, MVP, on the Release export template) belongs to the Graph; `CPU_BUDGET_MS` 0.5 (a median per frame) belongs to the Filter; `SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` 12 and `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` 10 to 25 belong to Sanity; `H_default = max(k, 3)` belongs to the Pipeline; `LIGHT_CPU_BUDGET_US` 1000 belongs to Lighting.

**Registry candidates that are not knobs** (written by the merge stage from this final text, never from a draft): `overlay_source_root` (path, placeholder `src/debug/`, every dev seam except the Overlay), `observer_source_root` (path, placeholder `src/observer/`, the Overlay and its sink; added at the re-review), `overlay_build_gate`, `dev_artifact_list`, `diag_panel_contract`, `expected_channels` and `observer_event_log` (added at design review), and the nine formulas `desync_classifier`, `graph_hook_tick`, `hook_window_p99`, `dirty_load_pm`, `queue_load_pm`, `ticks_per_overlay_refresh`, `overlay_cpu_us`, `hash_cost_per_s`, `stage_entry_latency_s`. The `DIAG_*` / `OVERLAY_*` knobs above are proposed as dev-presentation entries; ★ `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` is the only one that is a cross-system fact.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

The Overlay has **no world rendering of its own and no audio**. What is visible is a flat, unlit, text-first dev layer drawn by the Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera at pipeline layer 4 in the root viewport (integer-scaled at `H_default = max(k, 3)`, no Filter pass, no lighting, no camera jolt, no grade; DO-5), plus an optional set of world debug draws that the owners' channels toggle on the Pipeline's dev-only layer-0 debug node (DO-26). **Which GDD renders it:** the Pipeline owns the layer slot, the scale and the `DEBUG_OVERLAY` render-state modifier; this GDD owns the content (the pages, the tags, the palette use); each sibling owns the draw callback behind its own debug-draw toggle. **Art bible sections that apply:** section 3.4 (UI grammar: linear bars with a needle mark, never a ring, arc or dial), section 4.6 (the UI palette: Ground Graphite `#1C2128`, frame `#5C6670`, Hull Grey `#8C949C`, Paper White `#DDE4EA`, Tungsten `#FFCC66`, and Alarm Red `#E0102A` kept exclusively for alarm so that rarity is the signal, ab:375, ab:463), the reserved-hue scan (violet and flesh belong to sanity leaks and are never used here, ab:1207), R-7 (never an accessibility feature, ab:886) and ART 7.4 (the true-value overlay is never an accessibility option, restated at chart-table-web-map-and-menus.md:544).

| Event | Visual feedback | Audio feedback | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mode `OFF` to `FULL` (`toggle`) | The Graphite plate and the current page appear on the next rendered frame; no fade, no slide (DO-6) | None | MVP (dev) |
| Page change (`page_next`, `page_prev`) | The adjacent page replaces the previous on the next frame; the page name and viewer id stay in the header | None | MVP (dev) |
| A row becomes `OK` / `NOTE` / `WARN` | The text tag only (`OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN`) in Paper White; `WARN` uses no red and no fill | None | MVP (dev) |
| A row becomes `DEFECT` | The text tag `DEFECT` plus a small Alarm Red mark beside it; the mark is the only red on the page and is never a fill, bar or accent; a greyscale view still shows the tag (DO-27) | None (no beep, no sonification; the Player Fantasy guard rail "Never audible", DO-30) | MVP (dev) |
| `DELIBERATE` lie shown | `NOTE` tagged `LIE`: the true value in Paper White beside the perceived value in Hull Grey, never tinted by the lie, never violet or flesh (a lie is the game working, not a fault) | None | MVP (dev) |
| `NO_MASK` (`filter_pass_disabled`) | The `DEFECT` tag with the red mark (Pipeline pipe:735); `NOT_USED` is plain `OK`, never red | None | MVP (dev) |
| Kernel `FAULTED` in a dev build | The six-field FAULT record is pinned on `P-KERNEL` and on the strip; other pages freeze on their last good values; `HIDDEN` draws nothing (DO-11) | None | MVP (dev) |
| Kernel `LOADING` | A `LOADING` banner over the frozen last page | None | MVP (dev) |
| Observer strip shown (`mode`, or the `reveal` chord from `HIDDEN`) | One text line at `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR` on a Graphite plate: `T`, kernel state, Attention `phase`, sanity `stage`, hash status (DO-7) | None | MVP (dev) |
| Channel `REJECTED` / `SUSPENDED` / `STALE` | The status word and a `DEFECT` or `NOTE` row naming the `channel_id`; the last values stay visible, marked, when `STALE` | None | MVP (dev) |
| World debug draw toggled on (outlines, cones, rects, true positions) | Thin Hull Grey or Tungsten lines of at least `OVERLAY_DEBUG_DRAW_WIDTH_PX` 1 base pixel on the layer-0 dev node, drawn unshaded; toggled off, the world frame is bit-identical to a never-toggled frame Drawn only while the mode is `FULL`; leaving `FULL` hides the dev node and the toggles resume on re-entry (DO-26). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | None | MVP (dev) |
| Post-restore settle | `UNAVAILABLE / SETTLING` for `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` kernel ticks, shown as the class word | None | MVP (dev) |

**Visual rules carried from Detailed Rules.** Text-first pages, not floating windows; fixed-width digits; numerals refresh at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` while markers and debug draws update per rendered frame (DO-28); the Overlay never displays a value in a colour that follows the lie (DO-27); the Overlay is immune to hallucination by construction (it sits above the Filter pass and is fed nothing by it), so there is no "the tool goes mad at stage 3" beat; the register is clinical and literal: no in-fiction theming and no player-mode vocabulary ("developer mode", "cheat", "assist") anywhere on screen (see Player Fantasy).

**Audio.** None. The Overlay owns no audio node, plays no cue on any severity, and does not sonify counters. Adaptive Audio & Tells registers a diagnostics channel whose voice counters, identity table and bus meters are **displayed here and never played** (adaptive-audio-and-tells.md:1511: "the one place audio values may be shown"). The tells that precede a breach (Pillar 2) belong to the world and to Adaptive Audio, not to this tool.

**Assets.** No sprite, shader, animation or sound asset. One font requirement only: a tabular (fixed-width digit) face at the HUD scale, owned by whichever GDD fixes the project's UI typography (HUD & Suit Layer, designed, its single `Theme` at HS-48; *provisional until the dev layer adopts it*). The dev strings are English-only, are not in any player string table, and are excluded from the release export with the rest of the dev root (DO-2).

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:debug-overlay-true-values` to produce the per-asset visual specs; expect one tabular-digit font entry and no sprite, shader or audio assets.

## Game Feel

**Whose feel this is.** No player ever operates the Overlay, so there is no player feel to specify here: how a lie feels (the wrongness before the proof), how a breach feels (the tell, then chaos), how the HUD and instruments respond belong to the Perception Filter, the Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline, the Attention System, HUD & Suit Layer (designed) and In-World Instruments (designed (this run)), and Adaptive Audio & Tells. This section specifies the **operator's feel**: the one sane seat aboard (the developer, QA or playtest observer), whose tool must be as immediate and as unremarkable as a light switch. The player-facing feel this tool protects is the promise that a gauge and the frost beside it disagree for a reason (Pillar 1); the Overlay is how that is proved, not part of what is felt.

### Feel Reference

**Minecraft's F3 screen, for the hotkey**: one key, text on a flat plate, no gameplay effect, readable at a glance, gone when released from the mind. **A compact net-graph-style strip, for the observer**: a single line that can be watched without being read. **Anti-reference:** a floaty, animated, skinned dev console that fades, slides and bounces (every transition is a frame in which the operator cannot tell what is true), and any "true sight" or assist-mode feel (the Overlay must feel like a diagnostic instrument, never like a power the player holds; Player Fantasy, "Rejected, by name" paragraph).

### Input Responsiveness

| Action | Max input-to-response latency | Frame budget (60 fps) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `toggle` (`OFF` to `FULL`; from `FULL` or the strip the first press raises a confirm prompt and the second goes to `OFF`) | One rendered frame (DO-6: "takes effect within one rendered frame") | 1 frame | The page appears fully formed; no fade or slide. `T` does not change. |
| `page_next`, `page_prev` | One rendered frame | 1 frame | The page body changes; the numerals on it follow at the next refresh (at most 1 / `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` s). |
| `mode` and the `reveal` chord | One rendered frame | 1 frame | `HIDDEN` to the strip is a deliberate two-key action and never accidental. |
| `mark` | One rendered frame | 1 frame | The stamp reads `T` at the key press; a refused 9th on-screen mark shows a `NOTE`; in `HIDDEN` nothing is drawn and the `MARK` log line is flushed at once (DO-35 trigger (b)). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `dump` | One rendered frame to the confirmation line; the file is written in the same frame for the text size involved | 1 frame to confirm | A dump is a pure function of the model; two dumps at one `T` while `PAUSED` are byte-identical. |
| A numeral on a page | At most 1 / `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` = 100 ms behind the model; fold-fed numerals and the class cell at most one class fold plus one refresh (12 + 6 = 18 ticks) behind, at the 10 Hz rung | 6 frames at 10 Hz (18 ticks for fold-fed cells) | The refresh cadence keeps digits readable; peak-hold (DO-18) guarantees a one-tick spike is shown at the next refresh and never lost. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| A marker or debug-draw line | The same rendered frame | 1 frame | Markers and world debug draws follow the sim per frame, not per refresh. |

### Animation Feel Targets

Not applicable. The Overlay has no animation: no fade, slide, blink, pulse or easing on any element, because every animated frame is a frame the operator cannot trust as a reading, and an attention-grabbing flash on a `DEFECT` would break Pillar 2 for a tester in a shared room. State changes are instantaneous and text-only.

### Impact Moments

Not applicable. There is no hit-stop, screen shake, camera impact, controller rumble, time-scale change or sound, and none may be added (DO-30, the Player Fantasy guard rail "Never audible"). A `DEFECT` row's weight is carried by its tag text and its single red mark, not by any effect.

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

- **Weight**: light and flat. It adds no visible frame-time cost (`OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 1000 us per rendered frame, a dev-template figure, F-7); the world must not stutter when the Overlay opens, because a stutter would be misread as a defect it is meant to find.
- **Player control**: total. Every action is a single deliberate key; there is no mode in which the Overlay moves on its own, steals focus (DO-5), or changes the sim (DO-1).
- **Snap quality**: crisp and binary for modes and pages; steady, never jittering, for numerals (fixed-width digits; the digit column does not move between refreshes).
- **Acceleration model**: instant on, instant off.
- **Failure texture**: when the tool cannot see something it says so by name (`UNAVAILABLE` with a reason, `n/a`, `insufficient samples (n/N)`, "dev template: indicative"); it never shows a clean `HONEST` or `OK` for an input it lacks, and a lie it could not verify reads `UNVERIFIED` with the coverage readout beside `defects seen` (DO-21 (5)(v)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The operator should always be able to tell why a cell is empty.
- **Observer strip**: silent and peripheral; one line that can be watched from a seat away, with no sound and no change of state between refreshes.
- **Operator arc**: from "this report might be a defect" to "this row is `DELIBERATE`, the leak is present, the pipe is frosting" or to a named `DEFECT_*` with the field that proves it, inside one reproduction (Player Fantasy, fantasy failure condition).

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] Numerals on `P-KERNEL` and `P-TRUTH` do not jitter: the digit column's bounding box and glyph advance are pixel-identical in two captures taken 100 ms apart (AC-DO-80c).
- [ ] The Overlay's own frame cost stays under the per-frame ceiling on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (AC-DO-178, `[pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK]`, delegated ruling R12).
- [ ] The observer finds the Attention `TELL` transition in the observer log while the tester-screen scan finds no Overlay pixel (AC-DO-74).
- [ ] A `DELIBERATE` row and a `DEFECT_NO_LEAK` row are told apart by the tag text alone in a greyscale capture (AC-DO-81).

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the bullet that duplicated AC-DO-120 and the bullet about reviewers' adjectives, which no test can pass or fail, were deleted. The experiential test is the blind seeded-defect classification drill, AC-DO-158.)*

The cadence, cost and strip-placement figures cited in this section (`DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`, `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US`, `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR`) are Tuning Knobs rows, all `[PLACEHOLDER]`; this section restates none of them as a fact.

## UI Requirements

**No player-facing UI, and no UX flag.** The Overlay is a developer-only tool with no HUD, instrument, menu, screen or accessibility surface in any publicly released build (DO-2, DO-30; art bible R-7; the supervised playtest preset is the one exception, DO-6) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Because it is not a player surface, `/ux-design` is not requested for it and **no "UX Flag" line is raised** *(orchestrator ruling R26: the Overlay's dev surfaces are specified as dev-tool requirements below, the register used by the Horror and Attention GDDs, rather than as a UX spec)*. The player-facing surfaces that sit beside it are owned elsewhere: the HUD and Suit Layer (layer 2), the Chart Table and menus (layer 3), the In-World Instruments, and the Accessibility and Settings screens; none of them hosts, instantiates, parents or links to an Overlay node, and none may show an Attention level, a sanity stage, a lie count or a verdict (AC-AT-66, AC-PF-51, AC-SN-146). If a playtest ever asks for the Overlay "as an option", the answer is the Player Fantasy's rejected framing and DO-30, not a UI task.

**Dev-tool surface requirements** (the layer-4 `Control` tree; the model is pure, DO-4):

| Information | Display location | Update frequency | Condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Header: page name, viewer `PlayerId`, mode, kernel state, `T` (ticks and mm:ss), header severity (worst row on the page), the sticky `defects seen` count (DO-35) with the `unverified` count, the coverage readout and `N unclassified` when non-zero (DO-21 (5)(v)) | Top band of the Graphite plate, every page | Numerals at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; kernel state on event | `FULL` |
| The seven pages `P-KERNEL`, `P-HOOKS`, `P-STATE`, `P-TRUTH`, `P-SANITY`, `P-ATTENTION`, `P-FRAME`, and `P-CHANNELS` | One page at a time, text rows (row, value, unit, severity tag), fixed-width digits | Numerals at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; markers per rendered frame; `tick`-cadence figures peak-held | `FULL`; `page_next` / `page_prev` cycle and wrap |
| True value beside perceived value, class tag, `lie_id`, leak modes, `disc`, `tol_eff` | `P-TRUTH`, one block per selected ref, true in Paper White and perceived in Hull Grey | At `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; the active-lie marker per frame | A ref is listed (up to `DIAG_TRUTH_REFS_MAX`, "+n not shown") |
| Severity tag and (for `DEFECT`) the Alarm Red mark | Beside the row's value | Same as the row | The tag text always; the mark only for `DEFECT` and `NO_MASK` |
| The observer strip: `T`, kernel state, Attention `phase`, sanity `stage`, hash status | One line at `OVERLAY_STRIP_ANCHOR` (a corner) on a Graphite plate | At `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` | `OBSERVER_STRIP` (and `reveal` from `HIDDEN`); contains no ref, ledger field or pair |
| The FAULT record `(T, type, issuer, host_order, block_id, assertion)` | Pinned on `P-KERNEL` and on the strip | On event | Kernel `FAULTED` in a dev build; not drawn in `HIDDEN` |
| `LOADING` banner; boot progress and failure row | Over the frozen last page; on `P-KERNEL` | On event | Kernel `LOADING` or `BOOTING` |
| Channel list and the selected channel's rows; status word per channel | `P-CHANNELS` | At `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; status on event | `FULL`; statuses `OK`, `PENDING`, `STALE`, `ERROR`, `SUSPENDED`, `REJECTED` |
| Row cursor (a marker beside the selected row, plus the pinned-ref flag) | The current page, `P-TRUTH` and `P-CHANNELS` | Per rendered frame | `FULL`; moved by `cursor_next` / `cursor_prev`, acted on by `activate` (DO-8) |
| World debug-draw toggles (one per declared draw) | `P-CHANNELS`, beside the owning channel; the lines draw on the layer-0 dev node | Toggle on event; lines per frame | A channel declared the draw; dev build only |
| Dump confirmation line; refused-mark `NOTE` | Header band, one line | On event | After `dump` or a refused `mark` |

**Requirements, each a dev-tool constraint (not a UX spec):**

1. **Scale and layer.** Root-viewport `CanvasLayer` at pipeline layer 4, scaled by `H_default = max(k, 3)` plus `OVERLAY_SCALE_OFFSET` (default 0), so the baseline at 1280x720 is `H` = 3; above the HUD (2) and the chart table and menus (3); no Filter pass, lighting, jolt or grade (DO-5, AC-DO-109).
2. **Palette and tags.** Only the six palette colours of AC-DO-79; severity is a text tag first; Alarm Red only as a mark beside `DEFECT` and `NO_MASK`; `WARN` uses no red; no violet, no flesh, ever (DO-27).
3. **Legibility.** Fixed-width digits on an **opaque** Graphite plate (`OVERLAY_PLATE_ALPHA_PM` 1000); contrast measured against the worst-case composite over the world, which for an opaque plate is the plate itself, at least 4.5:1 for text and 3:1 for the red mark (a translucent plate over a white world measures under both floors, see Tuning Knobs); a cap-height floor of 12 physical px at 1280x720 `[PLACEHOLDER, confirm with UX]` (AC-DO-80).
4. **Focus and mouse.** The Overlay never takes gameplay focus; non-interactive panels use `MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE`; a click through the Overlay area reaches the game (`[REF modules/ui.md]`, AC-DO-76). The only interactive controls are the debug-draw toggles on `P-CHANNELS`, and they are reachable only by the Adapter-forwarded dev actions (DO-8: `cursor_next`, `cursor_prev`, `activate`), never by a rebindable input.
5. **Input.** Ten dev-only actions (one in the playtest preset: `debug_overlay_mark`, delegated ruling R16), forwarded host-locally by the Controller's Input Adapter under the dev predicate; never in the rebinding or accessibility enumerations (DO-8, AC-DO-18, AC-DO-70).
6. **Linear bars only.** Any bar is a linear bar with a needle mark; no ring, arc or dial (art bible 3.4, AC-DO-83).
7. **No audio, no motion.** No sound, no animation, no flash (Game Feel, the Player Fantasy guard rail "Never audible").
8. **Localization and accessibility.** Dev strings are English-only, outside every player string table, and absent from the release export; the Overlay is not offered as, and has no relationship to, any accessibility option (R-7); it appears in no screen-reader (AccessKit) tree of a release build because it is not in a release build, and in a dev build it adds no accessibility node (AC-DO-18).
9. **Evidence.** Retained screenshots of each screen touched (the seven pages and `P-CHANNELS`, the strip at two anchors, the `LOADING` banner, the `FAULTED` pin, a `REJECTED` and a `SUSPENDED` channel, and `HIDDEN`) in `production/qa/evidence/debug-overlay/` (AC-DO-80a to 82, 89, 152).

## Acceptance Criteria

Every criterion below is a GIVEN / WHEN / THEN a QA tester or a headless runner can pass or fail without opening this GDD's other sections: the terms it uses are defined in the **Test glossary**, the knob values it relies on are in the **Knob defaults** table, and the inputs of every classifier row are written out in full. Rule ids (`DO-n`), formula ids (`F-n`) and edge-case ids (`E-DO-nn`) are secondary tags in square brackets; sibling rules are named by their own id (Kernel CR-9, Filter PF-41, Sanity SN-28) and a `file:line` cite is a second tag only, because line numbers move on every sibling edit. The Overlay is an infrastructure system with no gameplay math of its own, so most criteria are Logic (automated, BLOCKING) and the player-visible ones are UI/Visual (a retained screenshot, BLOCKING at every `qa.level`).

**Tags.** `(auto)` an automated headless test in `tests/unit/debug_overlay/` or `tests/integration/debug_overlay/`. `(auto-gpu)` an automated test that needs rendered pixels or a real renderer (a draw-call count, a pixel diff, a composited glyph box): it runs only in the named windowed CI job `debug-overlay-windowed` (a real window and GPU, not the gdUnit4 headless job); a headless run reports it SKIPPED, never PASSED, and each such test first asserts that its draw count or pixel count is greater than 0, so it cannot pass vacuously at 0 draws *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. `(lint)` a source, asset or file-list scan run in CI. `(review)` a checklist with a **named reviewer**, retained in `production/qa/evidence/debug-overlay/`. `(manual)` retained evidence (screenshot, recording or log) in `production/qa/evidence/debug-overlay/`. `(spike)` a time-boxed engine-behaviour proof for an `[UNV, HIGH]` assumption (no engine-reference file exists for export, debugging or scripting). `[blocked: X]` marks a criterion that cannot pass until `X` exists; each has a stub-based interim test so the gate does not fall out silently (see Gaps). **Gate level:** `rigor: full` and `qa.level` unset, so Logic and Integration evidence is BLOCKING; UI and Visual evidence is BLOCKING at every level ("tests are waived, the look is not", coding standards); Config/Data rows are ADVISORY. **Spike rule:** each spike has a time box of one working day `[PLACEHOLDER]` and passes when its report file exists in `production/qa/spikes/` and states either "confirmed as designed" or a named ADR or GDD amendment request; a spike never silently changes a design rule.

**Determinism and harness (applies to every time-dependent row).** The test drives the Overlay's process callback with an **injected frame delta** (default 1/60 s) and steps the real kernel with its own tick driver; no criterion reads a wall clock, and the Overlay under test reads none (AC-DO-153). "N frames" means N calls of the process callback; "a refresh" means the callback on which the accumulated delta crosses `1 / DIAG_REFRESH_HZ`; "a fold" means the first callback after Sanity's `run` changes. Every classifier counter is in **kernel ticks** and advances by the ticks since the previous fold, never by callbacks, so a criterion that names a number of ticks does not change with the injected frame delta. **Fixtures are generated, not typed** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: reachable trajectories come from a committed script (`tests/fixtures/debug_overlay/gen_fold_trajectories.gd`, written with the story) that steps the counters by fold arithmetic (12 ticks per fold, 15 on a catch-up fold, `dT` 0 on the first fold and after a restore); a value that fold arithmetic cannot reach (an `n_eq` that is not a multiple of 12, a `t_ret` that is not a multiple of 0.2 s) is labelled **predicate probe** in the row and tests the predicate only. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Seams requested of the lead-programmer, **a story-readiness precondition**: `/story-readiness` marks an Overlay story BLOCKED until the seam it needs exists, recorded in the debug-gate ADR (the fallbacks in Gaps are interim, not a waiver) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (not decided here; each has the fallback in Gaps): **T-1** an injectable dev-tag provider in the composition root; **T-2** the kernel exposing the calling subscriber id at mutator and `WriteScope` entry; **T-3** the injectable frame delta and fake clock above; **T-4** stubbable Filter (`perceived`, counters, `t_return_max`, `q_display`), Sanity ledger and Graph/Suit `true_value` sources behind the owners' published read views; **T-5** a harness hook that forces `state_hash` every tick independent of the Overlay (Kernel G4); **T-6** call-spy seams on the Filter's `perceived` and on the Overlay's handler and classifier; **T-7** an injected `ObserverSink` (the log writer, with a recording stub) so the model does no file I/O (DO-4, DO-35) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.

**Test glossary** (one line per term; the definition is the one in Formulas F-1).

| Term | Meaning in these criteria |
|---|---|
| `tv`, `perceived`, `displayed_value` | `tv` is the owner's `true_value`; `perceived` is the Filter's `Perceived` record for `(ref, viewer)`; `displayed_value` is its value, equal to `tv` exactly on the honest path |
| `disc`, `p_int` | `disc = abs(p_int - tv)` in the ref's own unit (`bp`, `mC`, `dkPa`, `permille`); `p_int` is the honest `displayed_value` itself in `HONEST` mode, else `floor_div(value_lo x 1000 + span x displayed_pm + 500, 1000)` from the integer `displayed_pm`; 0 or 1 for binary-frame refs *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `tol(kind)`, `q_display`, `tol_eff` | `tol` is the owner's dirty-key quantum for the field (Graph `hp` 250 bp, `T` 1000 mC, `P` 10 dkPa, `p_o2` 5 dkPa, store 20 permille, intensity 100 permille; Suit reserve and cell 5 percent of max, `integrity_bp` 250 bp; 0 for `o2_pm`, `n_o2`, `n_inert`); `q_display` is one per mille of the instrument range (Filter PF-45 calibration, published by the Filter, registry `filter_counters`); `tol_eff = max(tol, q_display)`; `disc == tol_eff` is **inside** |
| `ledger_active` | the viewer's `sanity.ledger` holds a live row (state `ACTIVE`, listed for the viewer by `ledger_listed`, `away` or not) matching the ref's own `subject_ref` (subject granularity in `SUBJECT_SCOPE`); Sanity `PENDING` and the ended states are not active *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `lie_age`, `reach_age` | `lie_age = run - active_run` in Sanity runs (5 per second); `reach_age = ceil(600 x (end_run - active_run) / 1000)` runs (`GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` 0.6); binary-frame refs use `SWAP_LEAD_S` x 5 runs |
| `n_eq`, `n_skew`, `t_ret`, `t_stall` | kernel ticks (advanced at folds by the ticks since the previous fold, only when `T` changed) with `disc <= tol_eff` and a visible departure intended, counted only from the first fold on which `lie_age >= reach_age` (0 before it; binary-frame refs: from `SWAP_LEAD_S` x 5 runs); kernel ticks with `ledger_active` XOR (`lie_id != 0` and `motion_mode != RETURN`); seconds since `RETURN` began; seconds since the displayed value last moved toward the true value by at least `q_display` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `t_return_max(lie)` | the longest an honest return may go without progress (the stall bound), published by the Filter per lie (depends on `rm`); the Overlay never restates it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Classes | `UNAVAILABLE`, `SKEW`, `DEFECT_NO_LEAK`, `DEFECT_SILENT_LIE`, `DEFECT_BYPASS`, `DELIBERATE` (first tag `LIE`, or `UNVERIFIED` with its why `INTENT_UNKNOWN` or `SUBJECT_SCOPE`; second tag `ONSET`, `SHORT_REACH`, `SUB_TOL` or `NO_NEEDLE`), `RETURNING`, `HONEST`, `DEFECT_UNCAUSED` (with a `why`); severity words are exactly `OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN`, `DEFECT` |
| `P-KERNEL`, `P-HOOKS`, `P-STATE`, `P-TRUTH`, `P-SANITY`, `P-ATTENTION`, `P-FRAME`, `P-CHANNELS` | the seven MVP pages and the generic channel page (DO-15) |
| Modes | `OFF`, `FULL`, `OBSERVER_STRIP`, `HIDDEN` (DO-6) |
| Fold, `lie_intent` | a fold is the model's classification step, at most once per change of `run`; `lie_intent` is the Filter's per-ref record `{d_intended_pm, short_reach, not_drawn}` (F-1) |

**Knob defaults used by these criteria** (all `[PLACEHOLDER]`, from Tuning Knobs; a criterion that names a knob uses this value).

| Knob | Value | Knob | Value |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` | 10 | `DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S` | 5 s |
| `DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS` | 120 | `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` | 3 |
| `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS` | 24 | `DIAG_TRUTH_REFS_MAX` | 16 |
| `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` | 12 | `DIAG_CMD_LOG_ROWS` | 12 |
| `DIAG_HOOK_WINDOW_RUNS` / `DIAG_P99_MIN_SAMPLES` | 600 / 100 | `DIAG_DUMP_MAX_FILES` / `DIAG_MARK_MAX` | 20 / 8 |
| `DIAG_DIRTY_WARN_PM` / `DIAG_DIRTY_ALERT_PM` | 500 / 750 | `DIAG_FRAME_WARN_FPS` | 20 |
| `DIAG_QUEUE_WARN_PM` / `DIAG_LAG_WARN_TICKS` | 500 / 4 | `DIAG_FILTER_CPU_WINDOW_FRAMES` | 300 |
| `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` | 1000 | `DIAG_SELF_COST_WINDOW_FRAMES` | 300 |
| `DIAG_NO_LEAK_GRACE_TICKS` | 24 | `DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS` | 300 |
| `DIAG_RETURN_FLOOR_QD` | 5 | `DIAG_OFF_CONFIRM_S` | 3 s |
| `OVERLAY_FRAME_CEILING_US` | 2000 | `DIAG_HOOK_MAX_BREACH_RUNS` | 2 |
| `DIAG_DEV_TEMPLATE_SCALE_PM` | 0 | `DIAG_DEFECT_RING_MAX` | 64 |
| `DIAG_REFRESH_LADDER_HZ` | {10, 5, 2} | `OVERLAY_PLATE_ALPHA_PM` | 1000 |

**Fixtures.** `FX-REPLAY-A`: a scripted, seeded run of 1800 ticks (30 s) on the MVP slice (home-ship 5 compartments, 8 faults, one `FALSE_OK` lie active between tick 600 and tick 1200), run Overlay-absent, `HIDDEN` and `FULL`, with every page cycled every 60 frames. `FX-CLASS`: the classifier fixture set of Formulas F-1 (Graph `hp`, `tol` 250 bp, `q_display` 10 bp, `tol_eff` 250; `P` in dkPa; `T` in mC), one data table, one row per class, the runner reporting the failing row id (the AC-PF-40 pattern); its expected classes are the F-1 example rows. The `hp` dial is a synthetic fixture: Instruments holds `strain` (hp) out of the MVP manifest (in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md:227), so the MVP live refs are `p_o2`, `P`, `T`, `stock`, `load`. `FX-RETURN`: Graph `hp` 0 to 10000 bp (1 per mille = 10 bp, so `q_display` is 10, `p_int` = 10 x `displayed_pm` and every displayed value is a multiple of 10), needle `arc_deg` 240 (Instruments IW rule at in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md:201) so 1 deg = 41.67 bp, `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` 3.0 deg/s = 125 bp/s = 25 bp per class fold (104 bp/s = 20.8 bp per fold at `rm` 0.6, `rm_ret` 0.83), `disc_end` 2500 bp at removal (`displayed_pm` 490), `tv` 2400 held unless a row says otherwise, one class fold per 12 ticks. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: re-based on grid-valid sequences; the earlier values 4162.5 and 4787 cannot be produced by an integer `displayed_pm`.)* `FX-SOAK`: a scripted 600 s run (36 000 ticks) of the MVP slice with Filter, Sanity and Graph stubs that are deterministic in `T`, a lie of every kind and eligibility (GLIDE on a needle with room, `FALSE_OK` and `FALSE_FAIL` on a needle and on a housing frame, `SPRITE_SWAP`, a short-reach dial, an ineligible needle) issued and ended at fixed ticks, one restore at tick 18 000 and one 3 s pause at tick 24 000, run at injected frame deltas of 1/20, 1/60 and 1/144 s. The Sanity stub also publishes `ledger_listed` (every `ACTIVE` row listed, `away` or not, SN-23), and three lies are added on their own refs *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*: **L-A** (a short absence): `active_run` 2100 (tick 25 200), life 500 runs, `end_run` 2600 (tick 31 200); the seat leaves the subject's compartment at run 2450 (tick 29 400) and returns at run 2475 (tick 29 700), 300 ticks = 5 s away, the row `ACTIVE` and listed throughout. **L-B** (an end while away): `active_run` 2200 (tick 26 400), life 600 runs, `end_run` 2800 (tick 33 600); the seat leaves at run 2760 (tick 33 120) and returns at run 2830 (tick 33 960), so the lie ends while the seat is away. **L-C** (an end on one gauge of a three-gauge compartment): a lie on `T` of compartment `c2` (refs `T`, `P`, `p_o2`), `active_run` 2250 (tick 27 000), life 250 runs, `end_run` 2500 (tick 30 000); the run is made twice, with the Filter stub publishing `lie_targets` and then withholding it. `FX-SEEDED`: the same run with exactly one seeded defect per run, one run per class: a gleam the Filter stub asks for and never draws (`DEFECT_NO_LEAK`), a lie the stub intends and never draws (`DEFECT_SILENT_LIE`), a node that draws an angle other than `perceived` implies (`DEFECT_BYPASS`; run with the render-probe stub, since a bypass is visible only to the probe, DO-22), a stale value with no ledger entry (`DEFECT_UNCAUSED`).

### 3. The classifier `desync_classifier` (DO-20 to DO-23; formula F-1)

Test file `tests/unit/debug_overlay/test_desync_classifier.gd`; the runner reports the failing row id. Every row carries its full inputs (no row depends on a neighbour). The expected class of each row comes from the F-1 class table written independently of the code under test.

- **AC-DO-22 (auto)**: GIVEN `P` with `tv` 1013 dkPa, displayed 1013, `lie_id` 0, not `ledger_active` WHEN the classifier runs THEN the class is `HONEST`, severity `OK`, no mark. `[F-1 example 1]`
- **AC-DO-23 (auto)**: GIVEN `T` with `tv` 21000 mC and displayed 21001 (1 mC off, far inside `tol` 1000), `lie_id` 0, not `ledger_active` WHEN the classifier runs on two consecutive folds THEN the first reads `UNAVAILABLE` / `MEMO_UNCONFIRMED` and the second is `DEFECT_UNCAUSED` / `DEFECT`, proving `HONEST` is exact equality and never `tol`. `[pf:247; F-1 example 2]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### 5. The channel registration contract (DO-12 to DO-14)

- **AC-DO-65 (auto)**: GIVEN a declaration with `channel_id` `fx_channel`, owner `Fixture`, and three rows (name, unit, kind in {`COUNTER`, `GAUGE`, `FLAG`, `TEXT`, `TABLE`}, severity rule) WHEN registered in `BOOTING` THEN `P-CHANNELS` lists it and renders each row with the declared unit. Table-driven negatives, one row per invalid declaration, each yields status `REJECTED` with an error row naming the channel and the fault: a missing `channel_id`; a duplicate `channel_id`; an unknown row kind (`GRAPH`); a unit embedded in a row name (`fuel_ms`) when the owner's registry name does not carry it; a name breaking the snake_case / owner-prefix convention (`FuelMs!`); a registration whose `pull` Callable is invalid (`INVALID_CALLABLE`). The Overlay does not crash and the other channels render. `[DO-12, DO-13]`
- **AC-DO-66 (auto)**: GIVEN a channel registered in `BOOTING` WHEN the Overlay is opened at any later time THEN the channel appears with its rows; GIVEN a registration attempted after `BOOTING` (the BOOTING-only window is this GDD's own rule, adopted by analogy with Kernel CR-6, which covers blocks, command types and hooks only) THEN it is refused and never appears, and the expected-channel list (the `expected_channels` data list of AC-DO-69) shows `NOTE absent` for it; GIVEN an expected channel not yet registered while the kernel is still `BOOTING` THEN it shows `PENDING`, and once `BOOTING` ends it shows `NOTE absent` and a registration attempt returns `{ok: false, reason: "LATE_REGISTRATION"}` (`PENDING` has no late exit). `[E-DO-38; DO-12; DO-14]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-DO-67 (auto)**: GIVEN a severity rule that names an unknown severity word (`ERROR`) WHEN registered THEN the channel is `REJECTED`; only `OK`, `NOTE`, `WARN` and `DEFECT` exist; GIVEN a `DEFECT` rule that cites no owner rule THEN `REJECTED`; a `DEFECT` rule that cites an owner rule is accepted and evaluated. `[DO-13]`
- **AC-DO-68 (auto)**: GIVEN a channel whose `pull()` returns an invalid result (null) `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` 3 times in a row WHEN the model refreshes THEN one `DEFECT` row names the `channel_id` and `NULL_RESULT`, the other channels render, the channel is `SUSPENDED` until the next mode change, and the kernel stays `RUNNING`; the invalid-result table of AC-DO-09 is run against the latch as well. `[DO-14; Kernel CR-19]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-DO-69 (auto)**: GIVEN one registered channel per entry of the `expected_channels` data list (14 by default: Horror, Body, Lighting, Emitter, Layout, Save/Load, Interaction, Controller, Web Map, Audio, Assembly, Suit beyond `P-TRUTH`, In-World Instruments and HUD & Suit Layer) with stub rows WHEN `P-CHANNELS` is built THEN it lists exactly `len(expected_channels)` channels (the test reads the list, never a literal), selecting each shows its rows, a debug-draw toggle exists only for channels that declared one, and registering more than `DIAG_CHANNELS_MAX` 64 channels or `DIAG_ROWS_PER_CHANNEL_MAX` 64 rows yields `REJECTED` rows, not a crash; adding Boarding to the list adds one channel and changes no other criterion (Boarding is absent by the user's ruling of 2026-10-27, D98). `[DO-12, DO-26]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-DO-143 (auto)**: the channel status machine, with the injected clock: GIVEN a channel with no update for 4.9 s THEN `OK`; for 5.0 s (`DIAG_CHANNEL_STALE_S`) THEN `STALE` / `NOTE` with the last values marked; WHEN the next valid `pull()` succeeds THEN `OK`. GIVEN `pull()` returns an invalid result twice and then a valid one THEN the channel is `OK` and not suspended (the failure count resets); GIVEN it returns invalid results three times in a row THEN `SUSPENDED` with a `DEFECT` row, it stays suspended through further refreshes, and the next mode change resumes pulling. `[DO-14; channel status machine]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

Wall-clock rows are `[pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK]`: the two reference machines are named (delegated ruling R12, 2026-10-28; `project.yaml` `qa.min_spec`: RM-DESKTOP, the minimum-spec desktop with a 16.6 ms P99 CPU frame ceiling, and RM-DECK, the Steam Deck LCD at 30 fps with a 33.3 ms ceiling), and each figure is measured at the spike on the **playtest preset's release export** (Godot 4.7.2 release template, `forward_plus`, vsync on, the Pipeline's 120-frame warm-up, the startup read-back of renderer and driver recorded with each result), which is R12's build type for the one build that contains the Overlay. The developer's machine is indicative only. The Overlay's numbers gate nothing in the public Release, where it is absent. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*

### 10. Sibling acceptance criteria this GDD makes runnable (none re-owned)

- **AC-DO-99 (auto)**: kern:915: GIVEN a `DIAGNOSTIC` subscriber (the Overlay's) WHEN one tick runs THEN it reads `T`, kernel state, total and per-issuer queue depth, the last-N command log with results and reasons, the resolved hook order with per-hook timings and the over-budget flag, rejection counters by reason, the lag counter, and `state_hash` with `hash_T`. kern:915 now reads "refreshed at the diagnostics cadence" (applied 2026-10-27, DO-33): the criterion asserts that `state_hash` with its `hash_T` is readable on every tick and is refreshed at `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` (not per tick), that `hash_T` names the tick the hash was computed at, and that the others refresh per tick. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### Gaps / untestable as written

1. **Wall-clock rows `[pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK]`.** AC-DO-94 (the mean and the window maximum, with AC-DO-178), the indicative AC-DO-95 and AC-DO-96, and the wall-clock half of AC-DO-107 become gates when the spike has measured them on the two reference machines named by delegated ruling R12 (`project.yaml` `qa.min_spec`), on the playtest preset's release export; until then they are indicative on the developer machine, and the Overlay's figure gates nothing in the public Release, where it is absent (DO-19) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*.
2. **Blocked on a neighbour amendment, each with an interim test.** AC-DO-71 and AC-DO-115 (the Controller amendment landed 2026-10-27, but the criteria leave `[blocked]` only after a green run against the real Adapter, OQ-DO-6; interim: a stub Adapter and the host-local entry point). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* AC-DO-5 (kernel exposes the calling subscriber id, seam T-2; interim: the writes-disabled enforcement for `DIAGNOSTIC`). AC-DO-134 (the Filter's ref-to-subject map, PF-9, is provisional; interim: a stub map). AC-DO-160, 163, 164 and 176 depend on the Filter, Sanity and Instruments publishing `lie_intent`, `lie_targets(ref)` and the render probes (OQ-DO-26); until then they run against stubs and the production classifier degrades to `UNVERIFIED` (why `INTENT_UNKNOWN` or `SUBJECT_SCOPE`) or `NO_RESOLVER`, never to a defect and never to the verified class. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* AC-DO-18 screen halves (Accessibility Settings and Settings & Input Rebinding have no file; interim: the action-map and node-tree enumerators).
3. **Subjective or process-bound evidence.** The tester-screen oracle risk is a process risk the code cannot remove: AC-DO-74b proves only that the scan of one 10-minute capture finds no Overlay pixel (AC-DO-74a proves the deterministic half on a scripted replay), and AC-DO-75 proves only that the protocol says so; a stream that shows `FULL` voids that session and no automated criterion can prevent it. AC-DO-80a to 80c are measurements (contrast against the opaque plate, cap height, jitter); the cap-height figure is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` to confirm with UX; the experiential test is the blind seeded-defect drill, AC-DO-158. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* AC-DO-81 depends on the art director's ratification of red-as-mark (OQ-DO-9).
4. **Design-time assumptions the criteria cannot confirm.** `DEFECT_BYPASS` has no real probe until IW-38's probe exists in a build (P-2): AC-DO-40 uses a stub, and the production check stays `ABSENT` (an unprobed bypass is not caught at all, and the coverage readout `bypass-probe 0/N` is the only signal, DO-20, AC-DO-184) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. The `SCANNER` ref has no true source and is `UNAVAILABLE / NO_TRUE_SOURCE` (OQ-DO-2). The throttle recovery rule of AC-DO-148 (two clean windows, backing off to four and eight) and the 10 percent, 0.05 ms and 64 KiB figures of AC-DO-95 and AC-DO-96 are placeholders proposed in OQ-DO-14, not adopted values.
5. **Spikes `(spike)` are engine-behaviour proofs, not behaviour criteria.** AC-DO-11, 12, 78, 86, 87, 181 and 192 answer `[UNV, HIGH]` questions (export filter, feature tags and the release template, second window, debug-draw colour under stage 3, render-info fields, hash cost, the AccessKit opt-out); each is time-boxed to one working day `[PLACEHOLDER]` and ends in a report or an ADR/GDD amendment request. The engine reference has no scripting, core, export or debugging file.
6. **Testability seams T-1 to T-7** (the preamble; T-7 is the injected observer sink, added at the re-review) are requests to the lead-programmer and, from the design review, a **story-readiness precondition** recorded in the debug-gate ADR: an Overlay story whose criteria need a seam is BLOCKED until the seam exists. Without T-1, T-3 and T-4 the integration group degrades to manual playtest evidence, which weakens the BLOCKING gate; without T-5 the inertness comparison depends on the kernel's own hash cadence. AC-DO-07 additionally depends on the Filter publishing its memo-hit counter (OQ-DO-28). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

## Open Questions

**User-decision items (first).** Autonomous mode answered every framing, approach and section-approval question by taking the recommended option and logging it; none is a blocking user question, but each of these is a decision the user may reverse, and none changes a pillar: (1) **Four modes** with `HIDDEN` as the playtest-session default and a two-step reveal (DO-6 to DO-8), accepting that a stream showing `FULL` or the strip voids a session; (2) **Alarm Red as a mark only**, never a fill (DO-27, ratification by the art director is OQ-DO-9); (3) **the Overlay is a display tool and writes nothing**, so `force_*` stays in the owners' debug APIs behind the dev console (DO-1); (4) **the kernel computes the hash, the Overlay displays it**, with a proposed kernel cadence knob (DO-17, DO-33); (5) **the classifier's closure rule**: a return that reaches `disc` 0 is `HONEST` on that frame even if `motion_mode` is still `RETURN` (DO-21, final authoring); (6) the rulings on the binary-frame age gate, `UNAVAILABLE` precedence, the static expected-channel list, mark overflow and the refresh-tick granularity of the stage stopwatch (DO-21, DO-31, E-DO-38, F-9); (7) the throttle sequence 10, 5, 2 Hz with a two-window recovery that backs off to four and eight windows after a failed step-up (E-DO-35, F-7). **Added at the 2026-10-27 design review (both since ruled):** (8) the **playtest export preset** (release template plus the `playtest` tag, `HIDDEN` forced, the single action `debug_overlay_mark`, the observer sink; the reveal chord, raised at the re-review as OQ-DO-30, is dev-only: Resolved 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R16 (user delegated all remaining calls): option A, the playtest preset registers only `debug_overlay_mark` and a tester session can never leave `HIDDEN`), which replaces the earlier ruling "a playtest build is a dev build launched `HIDDEN`; no third build flavour" (OQ-DO-25); (9) **dropping Boarding from the expected channels** (14, data-driven; OQ-DO-24). Both were applied in the design-review revision as working defaults and are reversible cheaply (a preset, a list entry). Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions): the user confirmed both on 2026-10-27 (D97 for (8), D98 for (9)); neither is a working default any more, and the earlier "no third build flavour in MVP" ruling is superseded by the user. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

Every other request in this section is made of an owner and is **not** a decision of this GDD: nothing here edits another system's file, the registry, the index or the concept document, and nothing in the Overlay's design depends on a request being granted (each has a stated fallback). Ids `OQ-DO-n` are cited from the rules, formulas, edge cases and acceptance criteria. **Column mapping:** *Owner* is the system or role that must answer (the table's "user / agent / GDD" owner kind: GDD means a neighbouring system's GDD, agent means a named specialist, user means the project owner); *Where it bites* names the rules and criteria affected and the target stage (next GDD, ADR, playtest or architecture) in its last words.

| Id | Question or request | Owner | Fallback until answered | Where it bites |
